`Sources/CreatorNodes/Fields/GreyImageDecoder.swift` (new file)

```swift
import CoreGraphics
import CreatorGraph
import Foundation
import ImageIO
import UniformTypeIdentifiers

/// Reads PNG and JPEG bytes into a `GreyImage` (fields spec §5). This file is the only user of ImageIO.
///
/// Images are untrusted input. `inspect` reads only the header and refuses a file over `ImageLimits.maxBytes`, a side over
/// `ImageLimits.maxSide`, any type but PNG and JPEG, and anything ImageIO cannot make sense of, all before a pixel is
/// decoded; `decode` then converts in bands of about 32 MB, so a legal 8,192 × 8,192 picture needs its 128 MB result and
/// not a second full-size bitmap, and checks for cancellation between bands.
///
/// Grey comes from the picture's own values: a monochrome file is read as it is; colour is converted to sRGB and then to
/// Rec. 709 luminance (0.2126 R + 0.7152 G + 0.0722 B) of the colour premultiplied by alpha, i.e. composited against black.
/// The EXIF/TIFF orientation tag (1 to 8) is applied: the grid is the picture as Preview shows it, and width and height swap for 5 to 8.
public enum GreyImageDecoder {
    /// The file formats that may be used.
    public enum Format: Sendable, Equatable {
        case png, jpeg

        /// The extension a file of this format is saved with.
        public var fileExtension: String { self == .png ? "png" : "jpg" }
    }

    /// What a header says.
    public struct Info: Sendable, Equatable {
        public let width: Int
        public let height: Int
        public let format: Format
    }

    /// How many bytes of 16-bit bitmap one band may take.
    static let defaultBandBytes = 32 * 1024 * 1024

    /// The format and size of `data` from its header, checked against the limits, without decoding any pixels.
    public static func inspect(_ data: Data) throws(ImageProblem) -> Info {
        guard data.count <= ImageLimits.maxBytes else { throw .tooManyBytes(data.count) }
        guard let source = makeSource(data) else { throw .undecodable }
        let info = try header(of: source)
        // ImageIO reads a PNG cut short as a black picture without complaint; a JPEG cut short it reports once decoded.
        guard info.format != .png || PNGStructure.isWhole(data) else { throw .undecodable }
        return info
    }

    /// The grey picture in `data`. Runs off the caller's actor; throws `ImageProblem` for a bad file and
    /// `CancellationError` when the task is cancelled.
    @concurrent
    public static func decode(_ data: Data) async throws -> GreyImage {
        try await decode(data, bandBytes: defaultBandBytes)
    }

    /// `decode(_:)` with the size of a band chosen, so a test can make a small picture take several bands.
    @concurrent
    static func decode(_ data: Data, bandBytes: Int) async throws -> GreyImage {
        try Task.checkCancellation()
        let info = try inspect(data)
        // ImageIO decodes a PNG whole each time a band is drawn from it (16 passes for a large colour picture, measured), so a PNG
        // is decoded once, up front, and the bands are drawn from that; a JPEG is read a band at a time and needs no such copy.
        let decodeOnce = info.format == .png
        let options = [kCGImageSourceShouldCache: decodeOnce, kCGImageSourceShouldCacheImmediately: decodeOnce] as CFDictionary
        guard let source = makeSource(data),
              let image = CGImageSourceCreateImageAtIndex(source, 0, options),
              image.width == info.width, image.height == info.height else { throw ImageProblem.undecodable }
        let pixels = try greyPixels(of: image, bandBytes: bandBytes)
        // A cut-short file can still hand back an image; the source knows it was not whole.
        guard CGImageSourceGetStatusAtIndex(source, 0) == .statusComplete,
              let grey = GreyImage(width: info.width, height: info.height, pixels: pixels) else { throw ImageProblem.undecodable }
        return grey
    }

    private static func makeSource(_ data: Data) -> CGImageSource? {
        CGImageSourceCreateWithData(data as CFData, [kCGImageSourceShouldCache: false] as CFDictionary)
    }

    private static func header(of source: CGImageSource) throws(ImageProblem) -> Info {
        guard let type = CGImageSourceGetType(source) as String? else { throw .undecodable }
        let format: Format
        switch type {
        case UTType.png.identifier: format = .png
        case UTType.jpeg.identifier: format = .jpeg
        default: throw .unsupportedType
        }
        guard CGImageSourceGetCount(source) >= 1,
              let properties = CGImageSourceCopyPropertiesAtIndex(source, 0, nil) as? [CFString: Any],
              let width = (properties[kCGImagePropertyPixelWidth] as? NSNumber)?.intValue,
              let height = (properties[kCGImagePropertyPixelHeight] as? NSNumber)?.intValue,
              width >= 1, height >= 1 else { throw .undecodable }
        guard width <= ImageLimits.maxSide, height <= ImageLimits.maxSide else { throw .tooManyPixels(width: width, height: height) }
        return Info(width: width, height: height, format: format)
    }

    /// Whether the picture is monochrome with no alpha, so its values can be read without a colour conversion.
    private static func isPlainGrey(_ image: CGImage) -> Bool {
        let hasAlpha: Bool
        switch image.alphaInfo {
        case .none, .noneSkipFirst, .noneSkipLast: hasAlpha = false
        default: hasAlpha = true
        }
        return image.colorSpace?.model == .monochrome && !hasAlpha
    }

    /// The picture's grey values, row 0 first, converted a band of rows at a time.
    private static func greyPixels(of image: CGImage, bandBytes: Int) throws -> [UInt16] {
        let width = image.width, height = image.height
        let plain = isPlainGrey(image)
        let components = plain ? 1 : 4
        let space = plain ? CGColorSpaceCreateDeviceGray() : CGColorSpace(name: CGColorSpace.sRGB)
        let alpha = plain ? CGImageAlphaInfo.none : CGImageAlphaInfo.premultipliedLast
        guard let space else { throw ImageProblem.undecodable }
        let bitmapInfo = alpha.rawValue | CGBitmapInfo.byteOrder16Little.rawValue
        let rowBytes = width * components * MemoryLayout<UInt16>.stride
        let bandRows = max(1, min(height, bandBytes / rowBytes))
        var pixels = [UInt16](repeating: 0, count: width * height)
        var top = 0
        while top < height {
            try Task.checkCancellation()
            let rows = min(bandRows, height - top)
            guard let context = CGContext(data: nil, width: width, height: rows, bitsPerComponent: 16, bytesPerRow: rowBytes,
                                          space: space, bitmapInfo: bitmapInfo),
                  let band = image.cropping(to: CGRect(x: 0, y: top, width: width, height: rows)),
                  let data = context.data else { throw ImageProblem.undecodable }
            context.draw(band, in: CGRect(x: 0, y: 0, width: width, height: rows))
            let raw = UnsafeBufferPointer(start: data.assumingMemoryBound(to: UInt16.self), count: width * rows * components)
            for index in 0..<(width * rows) {
                pixels[top * width + index] = plain
                    ? UInt16(littleEndian: raw[index])
                    : luminance(red: raw[index * 4], green: raw[index * 4 + 1], blue: raw[index * 4 + 2])
            }
            top += rows
        }
        return pixels
    }

    /// Rec. 709 luminance of premultiplied little-endian 16-bit components, rounded.
    private static func luminance(red: UInt16, green: UInt16, blue: UInt16) -> UInt16 {
        let weighted = 2126 * Int(UInt16(littleEndian: red)) + 7152 * Int(UInt16(littleEndian: green))
            + 722 * Int(UInt16(littleEndian: blue))
        return UInt16(min((weighted + 5000) / 10_000, 65_535))
    }
}
```

`Sources/CreatorNodes/Fields/PNGStructure.swift` (new file)

```swift
import Foundation

/// A PNG file's chunk chain, walked without decoding anything. ImageIO reads a PNG cut short in the middle of its data as an
/// all-black picture and says nothing, so a file is only trusted when its chunks lead to IEND.
enum PNGStructure {
    private static let signature: [UInt8] = [0x89, 0x50, 0x4E, 0x47, 0x0D, 0x0A, 0x1A, 0x0A]
    private static let end: [UInt8] = Array("IEND".utf8)

    /// Whether `data` is a PNG signature followed by whole chunks up to and including IEND. Bytes after IEND are ignored.
    static func isWhole(_ data: Data) -> Bool {
        data.withUnsafeBytes { bytes in
            guard bytes.count >= signature.count + 12, bytes.prefix(signature.count).elementsEqual(signature) else { return false }
            var offset = signature.count
            while offset + 12 <= bytes.count {
                let length = (0..<4).reduce(0) { $0 << 8 | Int(bytes[offset + $1]) }
                let next = offset + 12 + length  // length, length of type and CRC; a 32-bit length cannot overflow an Int
                guard next <= bytes.count else { return false }
                if bytes[(offset + 4)..<(offset + 8)].elementsEqual(end) { return true }
                offset = next
            }
            return false
        }
    }
}
```

### Task 4: Evaluation plumbing: the resolver, the cache key and the document's pool

How a node finds a picture's bytes, and how the evaluator notices that they changed. `ImageResolver` is a value (the document's embedded bytes and its folder) handed to every evaluation; `ImageStamp` is what a reference points at now, mixed into a node's cache key in `Evaluator.gather`. `DocumentModel` owns the session's pool of embedded images and the document's URL. `GraphFile.images` exists (in memory) from here; writing it is Task 6.

**Files:**

- Modify: `Sources/CreatorGraph/DocumentModel.swift`
- Modify: `Sources/CreatorGraph/EvalContext.swift`
- Modify: `Sources/CreatorGraph/Evaluator+Running.swift`
- Modify: `Sources/CreatorGraph/Evaluator.swift`
- Modify: `Sources/CreatorGraph/GraphFile.swift`
- Modify: `Sources/CreatorGraph/Groups/EvaluationSetup.swift`
- Create: `Sources/CreatorGraph/Images/ImageResolver.swift`
- Create: `Sources/CreatorGraph/Images/ImageStamp.swift`

Tests:

- Create: `Tests/CreatorGraphTests/Images/DocumentImageTests.swift`
- Create: `Tests/CreatorGraphTests/Images/EvaluatorImageTests.swift`
- Create: `Tests/CreatorGraphTests/Images/ImageResolverTests.swift`
- Create: `Tests/CreatorGraphTests/Support/ImageProbeNode.swift`
- Create: `Tests/CreatorGraphTests/Support/TempFolder.swift`

**Interfaces:**

- Consumes: `ImageReference`, `ImageHash`, `ImageLimits`, `ImageProblem`; `Evaluator.gather`, `EvaluationSetup`, `EvalContext`, `DocumentModel.scheduleEvaluation`.
- Produces: `ImageStamp` (`.embedded(hash, isPresent:)`, `.file(path:modified:size:)`, `.missing(path:)`, `.notAFile(path:)`); `ImageResolver(embedded:documentFolder:)` with `location(of:)`, `stamp(of:)`, `bytes(of:) throws(ImageProblem)`, `static bytes(ofFileAt:)`, `stored(_:inDocumentFolder:)`, `static relativePath(from:to:)`, internal `static read(_:name:)`; `ImageStamp.isKeepable`; `EvalContext.images`; `Evaluator.evaluate(_:definitions:demand:inspecting:images:)`; `DocumentModel.images`, `.documentURL`, `.addImage(_:) -> ImageHash`, `init(…documentURL:)`; `GraphFile(images:)`; `Evaluator.message(for:)` renders an `ImageProblem`; test fixtures `TempFolder`, `ImageProbeNode`.

**Note:** The stamp is read in `gather` on every evaluation (a `stat` per image node, up to three: the relative path, the symbolic-link resolution and the attributes). They run on the evaluator's actor, so a hung network volume or a dataless iCloud file can hold every evaluation up while the system call waits; that is the cost of D11's "checked at each evaluation" without a watcher (Risks). A failed node is never cached (`evaluate` throws, so `cache.insert` is not reached), which is why `GreyImageCache` (Task 5) also remembers pictures that cannot be decoded. Why the stamp and not a file watcher: spec D11 says "checked at each evaluation". `ImageResolver.bytes` refuses anything but a regular file before opening it, then opens it without blocking (`O_NONBLOCK`, so a path swapped for a named pipe between the check and the open cannot hold the evaluator) and checks the open descriptor with `fstat` again (`read(_:name:)`, tested directly with a real pipe: `aPathSwappedForANamedPipeAfterTheCheckIsRefusedNotWaitedOn`), and reads at most `maxBytes + 1` bytes, so `/dev/zero`, a pipe or a growing file cannot fill memory.

```swift
import CreatorKernel
import Foundation
import Testing
@testable import CreatorGraph

/// The document's pool of embedded images and the folder it is saved in, as `DocumentModel` hands them to every evaluation.
@MainActor
struct DocumentImageTests {
    let folder = TempFolder()

    func document(_ reference: ImageReference?, url: URL? = nil, images: [ImageHash: Data] = [:]) -> (DocumentModel, Node) {
        var node = EvaluatorImageTests.registry.makeNode(ImageProbeNode.typeID)
        node.isOutput = true  // a document evaluates what an output wants
        if let reference { node.inputValues[NodeSetting.image] = .image(reference) }
        let file = GraphFile(graph: Graph(nodes: [node.id: node]), images: images)
        return (DocumentModel(file: file, registry: EvaluatorImageTests.registry, kernel: FakeKernel(), documentURL: url), node)
    }

    func size(_ document: DocumentModel, _ node: Node) -> Int? {
        if case .one(.integer(let value))? = document.results[node.id]?.outputs?["size"] { value } else { nil }
    }

    @Test func addingImageBytesStoresThemOnceUnderTheirHashAndMakesAWaitingNodeRun() async throws {
        let data = Data("some picture".utf8)
        let (document, node) = document(.embedded(ImageHash(of: data)))
        await document.waitForEvaluation()
        if case .error? = document.results[node.id]?.state {} else { Issue.record("a node whose image is not held must fail") }
        let hash = document.addImage(data)
        #expect(hash == ImageHash(of: data) && document.addImage(data) == hash)
        #expect(document.images == [hash: data])
        await document.waitForEvaluation()
        #expect(size(document, node) == 12)
    }

    @Test func aFileOpenedWithImagesHoldsThemFromTheStart() async throws {
        let data = Data("held".utf8)
        let hash = ImageHash(of: data)
        let (document, node) = document(.embedded(hash), images: [hash: data])
        await document.waitForEvaluation()
        #expect(size(document, node) == 4 && document.images[hash] == data)
    }

    @Test func settingTheDocumentsURLRereadsRelativeLinks() async throws {
        try folder.write(Data(count: 6), to: "doc/vent.png")
        let (document, node) = document(.linked(relativePath: "vent.png", absolutePath: "/no/such/vent.png"))
        await document.waitForEvaluation()
        #expect(size(document, node) == nil, "unsaved: only the absolute path, which is wrong")
        document.documentURL = folder.url.appending(path: "doc/Plate.mcgraph")
        await document.waitForEvaluation()
        #expect(size(document, node) == 6)
    }
}
```

`Tests/CreatorGraphTests/Images/EvaluatorImageTests.swift` (new)

```swift
import CreatorKernel
import Foundation
import Testing
@testable import CreatorGraph

/// What the evaluator does with images (fields spec D11): the stamp is part of a node's cache key, so an edited file runs the
/// node again, an untouched one does not, and a failure is never cached.
struct EvaluatorImageTests {
    static let registry = NodeRegistry([ImageProbeNode.self])
    let folder = TempFolder()

    func probe(_ reference: ImageReference?) -> Node {
        var node = Self.registry.makeNode(ImageProbeNode.typeID)
        if let reference { node.inputValues[NodeSetting.image] = .image(reference) }
        return node
    }

    func size(_ report: EvaluationReport, _ node: Node) -> Int? {
        if case .one(.integer(let value))? = report.results[node.id]?.outputs?["size"] { value } else { nil }
    }

    func error(_ report: EvaluationReport, _ node: Node) -> String? {
        if case .error(let message)? = report.results[node.id]?.state { message } else { nil }
    }

    @Test func aNodeWithNoImageSaysSoAndASettingOnlyReachesTheNodeThroughItsReference() async throws {
        let evaluator = Evaluator(registry: Self.registry, kernel: FakeKernel())
        let node = probe(nil)
        let report = try await evaluator.evaluate(Graph(nodes: [node.id: node]), demand: [node.id])
        #expect(error(report, node) == "Image Field needs an image: choose one in the inspector.")
    }

    @Test func anEmbeddedImageIsCachedAndAnAbsentOneIsAnErrorThatIsNotCached() async throws {
        let evaluator = Evaluator(registry: Self.registry, kernel: FakeKernel())
        let data = Data("twelve bytes".utf8)
        let hash = ImageHash(of: data)
        let node = probe(.embedded(hash))
        let graph = Graph(nodes: [node.id: node])
        let absent = try await evaluator.evaluate(graph, demand: [node.id])
        #expect(error(absent, node) == ImageProblem.embeddedMissing.message)
        let held = ImageResolver(embedded: [hash: data])
        let first = try await evaluator.evaluate(graph, demand: [node.id], images: held)
        #expect(size(first, node) == 12 && first.evaluatedNodes == [node.id])
        let second = try await evaluator.evaluate(graph, demand: [node.id], images: held)
        #expect(size(second, node) == 12 && second.evaluatedNodes.isEmpty, "the same bytes hit the cache")
    }

    @Test func aLinkedFileRunsTheNodeAgainOnlyWhenItsModificationDateOrSizeChanges() async throws {
        let evaluator = Evaluator(registry: Self.registry, kernel: FakeKernel())
        let file = try folder.write(Data(count: 10), to: "vent.png")
        try folder.touch(file, Date(timeIntervalSince1970: 1_000_000))
        let node = probe(.linked(relativePath: nil, absolutePath: file.path(percentEncoded: false)))
        let graph = Graph(nodes: [node.id: node])
        let first = try await evaluator.evaluate(graph, demand: [node.id])
        #expect(size(first, node) == 10)
        #expect(try await evaluator.evaluate(graph, demand: [node.id]).evaluatedNodes.isEmpty, "untouched: reused")
        try Data(count: 25).write(to: file)
        try folder.touch(file, Date(timeIntervalSince1970: 2_000_000))
        let changed = try await evaluator.evaluate(graph, demand: [node.id])
        #expect(size(changed, node) == 25 && changed.evaluatedNodes == [node.id], "a change on disk is read at the next evaluation")
    }

    @Test func aMissingLinkFailsWithTheRelinkSentenceAndRecoversWhenTheFileAppears() async throws {
        let evaluator = Evaluator(registry: Self.registry, kernel: FakeKernel())
        let path = folder.url.appending(path: "textures/vent-mask.png")
        let node = probe(.linked(relativePath: nil, absolutePath: path.path(percentEncoded: false)))
        let graph = Graph(nodes: [node.id: node])
        let missing = try await evaluator.evaluate(graph, demand: [node.id])
        let message = try #require(error(missing, node))
        #expect(message.hasPrefix("Image 'vent-mask.png' wasn't found at ") && message.hasSuffix("/textures/. Relink it in the inspector."))
        try folder.write(Data(count: 7), to: "textures/vent-mask.png")
        let found = try await evaluator.evaluate(graph, demand: [node.id])
        #expect(size(found, node) == 7)
    }

    @Test func theDocumentsFolderDecidesWhereARelativeLinkPoints() async throws {
        let evaluator = Evaluator(registry: Self.registry, kernel: FakeKernel())
        try folder.write(Data(count: 3), to: "a/vent.png")
        try folder.write(Data(count: 5), to: "b/vent.png")
        let node = probe(.linked(relativePath: "vent.png", absolutePath: "/no/such/vent.png"))
        let graph = Graph(nodes: [node.id: node])
        let folderA = ImageResolver(documentFolder: folder.url.appending(path: "a"))
        let folderB = ImageResolver(documentFolder: folder.url.appending(path: "b"))
        let inA = try await evaluator.evaluate(graph, demand: [node.id], images: folderA)
        let inB = try await evaluator.evaluate(graph, demand: [node.id], images: folderB)
        #expect(size(inA, node) == 3 && size(inB, node) == 5, "saving the document elsewhere re-reads")
    }

    @Test func aNodeWithoutAnImageSettingIsNotAffected() async throws {
        let evaluator = Evaluator(registry: testRegistry, kernel: FakeKernel())
        let a = makeNode(ConstantNode.self, ["value": .number(1)])
        let g = graph([a])
        _ = try await evaluator.evaluate(g, demand: [a.id])
        let again = try await evaluator.evaluate(g, demand: [a.id], images: ImageResolver(documentFolder: folder.url))
        #expect(again.evaluatedNodes.isEmpty)
    }
}
```

`Tests/CreatorGraphTests/Images/ImageResolverTests.swift` (new)

```swift
import Foundation
import Testing
@testable import CreatorGraph

/// `ImageResolver` (fields spec §5, D11): where a reference's bytes are, whether they changed, and what is refused.
struct ImageResolverTests {
    let folder = TempFolder()
    let png = Data("not really a png, only bytes".utf8)

    func linked(_ file: URL, relative: String? = nil) -> ImageReference {
        .linked(relativePath: relative, absolutePath: file.path(percentEncoded: false))
    }

    // MARK: Embedded

    @Test func anEmbeddedImageIsFoundByItsHashAndReportsWhetherTheDocumentHoldsIt() throws {
        let hash = ImageHash(of: png)
        let held = ImageResolver(embedded: [hash: png])
        #expect(held.stamp(of: .embedded(hash)) == .embedded(hash, isPresent: true))
        #expect(try held.bytes(of: .embedded(hash)) == png)
        let empty = ImageResolver()
        #expect(empty.stamp(of: .embedded(hash)) == .embedded(hash, isPresent: false))
        #expect(throws: ImageProblem.embeddedMissing) { try empty.bytes(of: .embedded(hash)) }
        #expect(empty.location(of: .embedded(hash)) == nil)
    }

    // MARK: Linked files

    @Test func aLinkedFileIsReadAndItsStampChangesWhenItsDateOrSizeDoes() throws {
        let file = try folder.write(png, to: "vent.png")
        try folder.touch(file, Date(timeIntervalSince1970: 1_000_000))
        let resolver = ImageResolver()
        let reference = linked(file)
        let first = resolver.stamp(of: reference)
        let path = file.path(percentEncoded: false)
        #expect(first == .file(path: path, modified: Date(timeIntervalSince1970: 1_000_000), size: png.count))
        #expect(try resolver.bytes(of: reference) == png)
        #expect(resolver.stamp(of: reference) == first, "unchanged")
        try folder.touch(file, Date(timeIntervalSince1970: 2_000_000))
        #expect(resolver.stamp(of: reference) != first, "a new modification date, same bytes")
        try folder.touch(file, Date(timeIntervalSince1970: 1_000_000))
        try Data("longer than before, by far".utf8).write(to: file)
        try folder.touch(file, Date(timeIntervalSince1970: 1_000_000))
        #expect(resolver.stamp(of: reference) != first, "the same date, another size")
    }

    @Test func theRelativePathIsTriedFirstThenTheAbsoluteOne() throws {
        let beside = try folder.write(Data("beside".utf8), to: "doc/maps/vent.png")
        let elsewhere = try folder.write(Data("elsewhere!".utf8), to: "other/vent.png")
        let reference = ImageReference.linked(relativePath: "maps/vent.png", absolutePath: elsewhere.path(percentEncoded: false))
        let docFolder = folder.url.appending(path: "doc", directoryHint: .isDirectory)
        #expect(try ImageResolver(documentFolder: docFolder).bytes(of: reference) == Data("beside".utf8))
        #expect(try ImageResolver().bytes(of: reference) == Data("elsewhere!".utf8), "an unsaved document has no folder")
        try FileManager.default.removeItem(at: beside)
        #expect(try ImageResolver(documentFolder: docFolder).bytes(of: reference) == Data("elsewhere!".utf8), "the fallback")
    }

    @Test func aMissingFileSaysWhereItLookedAndLeavesWorkToTheInspector() throws {
        let gone = folder.url.appending(path: "textures/vent-mask.png")
        let reference = linked(gone)
        let resolver = ImageResolver()
        #expect(resolver.stamp(of: reference) == .missing(path: gone.path(percentEncoded: false)))
        let textures = folder.url.appending(path: "textures", directoryHint: .isDirectory)
        let folderPath = textures.standardizedFileURL.path(percentEncoded: false)
        #expect(throws: ImageProblem.notFound(name: "vent-mask.png", folder: folderPath)) { try resolver.bytes(of: reference) }
        let message = ImageProblem.notFound(name: "vent-mask.png", folder: folderPath).message
        #expect(message.hasSuffix("textures/. Relink it in the inspector."))
    }

    @Test func aFolderOrADeviceIsNotAFileAndIsNeverOpened() throws {
        try folder.write(png, to: "maps/inside.png")
        for path in [folder.url.appending(path: "maps").path(percentEncoded: false), "/dev/zero", "/dev/null"] {
            let reference = ImageReference.linked(relativePath: nil, absolutePath: path)
            let standard = URL(filePath: path).standardizedFileURL.path(percentEncoded: false)
            #expect(ImageResolver().stamp(of: reference) == .notAFile(path: standard))
            #expect(throws: ImageProblem.notAFile(name: reference.fileName ?? "")) { try ImageResolver().bytes(of: reference) }
        }
    }

    /// The stamp saw a file, then the path became a named pipe: `read` opens without blocking and checks what it opened, so it
    /// answers at once instead of waiting for a writer that never comes. (A regular file and a pipe cannot be told apart by a
    /// test that goes through `bytes(of:)`, which checks the path first; this one calls the reader itself.)
    @Test(.timeLimit(.minutes(1))) func aPathSwappedForANamedPipeAfterTheCheckIsRefusedNotWaitedOn() throws {
        let pipe = folder.url.appending(path: "swapped.png")
        #expect(mkfifo(pipe.path(percentEncoded: false), 0o644) == 0)
        #expect(throws: ImageProblem.notAFile(name: "swapped.png")) { try ImageResolver.read(pipe, name: "swapped.png") }
        let real = try folder.write(png, to: "real.png")
        #expect(try ImageResolver.read(real, name: "real.png") == png)
        #expect(throws: ImageProblem.self) { try ImageResolver.read(folder.url.appending(path: "none.png"), name: "none.png") }
    }

    @Test func aSymbolicLinkToAFileIsFollowed() throws {
        let target = try folder.write(png, to: "real.png")
        let link = folder.url.appending(path: "link.png")
        try FileManager.default.createSymbolicLink(at: link, withDestinationURL: target)
        #expect(try ImageResolver().bytes(of: linked(link)) == png)
    }

    @Test func aFileOverSixteenMegabytesIsRefusedUnreadAndOneAtTheLimitIsRead() throws {
        let big = try folder.write(Data(count: ImageLimits.maxBytes), to: "exact.png")
        #expect(try ImageResolver().bytes(of: linked(big)).count == ImageLimits.maxBytes)
        let handle = try FileHandle(forWritingTo: big)
        try handle.truncate(atOffset: UInt64(ImageLimits.maxBytes + 1))
        try handle.close()
        #expect(throws: ImageProblem.tooManyBytes(ImageLimits.maxBytes + 1)) { try ImageResolver().bytes(of: linked(big)) }
    }

    @Test func bytesOfAChosenFileAreBoundedLikeAnyOther() throws {
        let file = try folder.write(png, to: "a.png")
        #expect(try ImageResolver.bytes(ofFileAt: file) == png)
        #expect(throws: ImageProblem.self) { try ImageResolver.bytes(ofFileAt: folder.url.appending(path: "nope.png")) }
        #expect(throws: ImageProblem.self) { try ImageResolver.bytes(ofFileAt: folder.url) }
    }

    @Test func readingAndStampingWriteNothingBesideTheImage() throws {
        let file = try folder.write(png, to: "maps/vent.png")
        let before = folder.listing
        let resolver = ImageResolver(documentFolder: folder.url)
        _ = resolver.stamp(of: linked(file))
        _ = try resolver.bytes(of: linked(file))
        _ = resolver.stored(linked(file), inDocumentFolder: folder.url)
        #expect(folder.listing == before)
    }

    // MARK: Relative paths and saving

    @Test func relativePathsGoDownUpAndAcross() {
        let root = URL(filePath: "/work/project", directoryHint: .isDirectory)
        func relative(_ folder: String, _ file: String) -> String {
            ImageResolver.relativePath(from: root.appending(path: folder, directoryHint: .isDirectory), to: root.appending(path: file))
        }
        #expect(relative("doc", "doc/vent.png") == "vent.png")
        #expect(relative("doc", "doc/maps/vent.png") == "maps/vent.png")
        #expect(relative("doc", "vent.png") == "../vent.png")
        #expect(relative("doc/deep", "shared/vent.png") == "../../shared/vent.png")
        #expect(relative("doc", "docs/vent.png") == "../docs/vent.png", "a shared name prefix is not a shared folder")
    }

    @Test func savingWritesBothPathsOfAFoundFileAndKeepsAMissingOnesAsTheyWere() throws {
        let file = try folder.write(png, to: "doc/maps/vent.png")
        let docFolder = folder.url.appending(path: "doc", directoryHint: .isDirectory)
        let resolver = ImageResolver()
        let unsaved = linked(file)
        let stored = resolver.stored(unsaved, inDocumentFolder: docFolder)
        #expect(stored == .linked(relativePath: "maps/vent.png", absolutePath: file.standardizedFileURL.path(percentEncoded: false)))
        let moved = resolver.stored(stored, inDocumentFolder: folder.url.appending(path: "elsewhere", directoryHint: .isDirectory))
        #expect(moved == .linked(relativePath: "../doc/maps/vent.png", absolutePath: file.standardizedFileURL.path(percentEncoded: false)),
                "Save As elsewhere: the relative path is made again from the new folder")
        let missing = ImageReference.linked(relativePath: "gone/x.png", absolutePath: "/no/such/x.png")
        #expect(resolver.stored(missing, inDocumentFolder: docFolder) == missing)
        let hash = ImageHash(of: png)
        #expect(resolver.stored(.embedded(hash), inDocumentFolder: docFolder) == .embedded(hash))
        let absolute = file.standardizedFileURL.path(percentEncoded: false)
        #expect(resolver.stored(unsaved, inDocumentFolder: nil) == .linked(relativePath: nil, absolutePath: absolute))
    }
}
```

`Tests/CreatorGraphTests/Support/ImageProbeNode.swift` (new)

```swift
// Test fixture file: a node that reads its `image` setting through the evaluation's image resolver.
import CreatorKernel
@testable import CreatorGraph

/// Outputs the number of bytes behind its `image` setting, so a test sees when the bytes, or the evaluation, change.
enum ImageProbeNode: NodeDefinition {
    static let typeID = "test.imageProbe"
    static let displayName = "Image Probe"
    static let category = NodeCategory.value
    static let inputs: [SocketSpec] = []
    static let outputs = [SocketSpec("size", .integer)]

    static func evaluate(_ inputs: NodeInputs, kernel: any Kernel, context: EvalContext) async throws -> NodeOutputs {
        guard case .image(let reference)? = context.node.inputValues[NodeSetting.image] else { throw ImageProblem.notChosen }
        return NodeOutputs(["size": .integer(try context.images.bytes(of: reference).count)])
    }
}
```

`Tests/CreatorGraphTests/Support/TempFolder.swift` (new)

```swift
// Test fixture file: a folder under the temporary directory that removes itself, for the tests that link to real files.
import Foundation

final class TempFolder {
    let url: URL

    init() {
        url = URL.temporaryDirectory.appending(path: "mc-images-\(UUID().uuidString)", directoryHint: .isDirectory)
        try? FileManager.default.createDirectory(at: url, withIntermediateDirectories: true)
    }

    deinit { try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: url) }

    /// Writes `data` at `name` (a path under the folder, its folders made) and returns the file's URL.
    @discardableResult
    func write(_ data: Data, to name: String) throws -> URL {
        let file = url.appending(path: name)
        try FileManager.default.createDirectory(at: file.deletingLastPathComponent(), withIntermediateDirectories: true)
        try data.write(to: file)
        return file
    }

    /// Sets a file's modification date.
    func touch(_ file: URL, _ date: Date) throws {
        try FileManager.default.setAttributes([.modificationDate: date], ofItemAtPath: file.path(percentEncoded: false))
    }

    /// The names of everything under the folder, sorted: what "nothing was written beside it" compares.
    var listing: [String] {
        let paths = FileManager.default.enumerator(atPath: url.path(percentEncoded: false))?.allObjects as? [String] ?? []
        return paths.sorted()
    }
}
```

`Sources/CreatorGraph/Images/ImageResolver.swift` (new file)

```swift
import Foundation

/// Finds the bytes behind an `ImageReference` (fields spec §5): the embedded images the document holds, and, for a linked
/// one, the file at its path. A value, handed to every evaluation (`Evaluator.evaluate(_:images:)`, `EvalContext.images`);
/// it decodes nothing (`GreyImageDecoder` does, in CreatorNodes).
///
/// A linked path is tried relative to the document's folder first, then as the absolute fallback; nothing is ever written
/// beside a linked image, and a path that names anything but a regular file (a folder, a device, a pipe) is refused before it
/// is opened, so a hand-edited document cannot make the app read `/dev/zero`.
public struct ImageResolver: Sendable {
    /// The document's embedded images by hash.
    public var embedded: [ImageHash: Data]
    /// The folder the document lives in; `nil` while it is unsaved.
    public var documentFolder: URL?

    public init(embedded: [ImageHash: Data] = [:], documentFolder: URL? = nil) {
        self.embedded = embedded
        self.documentFolder = documentFolder
    }

    // MARK: Where a linked file is

    /// The place a linked reference points at now: the relative path under the document's folder when something is there, else
    /// the absolute path (whether or not anything is there). `nil` for an embedded image.
    public func location(of reference: ImageReference) -> URL? {
        guard case .linked(let relative, let absolute) = reference else { return nil }
        if let relative, let folder = documentFolder {
            let candidate = folder.appending(path: relative).standardizedFileURL
            if FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: candidate.path(percentEncoded: false)) { return candidate }
        }
        return URL(filePath: absolute).standardizedFileURL
    }

    /// What `reference` points at now (see `ImageStamp`).
    public func stamp(of reference: ImageReference) -> ImageStamp {
        switch reference {
        case .embedded(let hash):
            return .embedded(hash, isPresent: embedded[hash] != nil)
        case .linked:
            guard let url = location(of: reference) else { return .missing(path: "") }
            let path = url.path(percentEncoded: false)
            let target = url.resolvingSymlinksInPath().path(percentEncoded: false)
            guard let attributes = try? FileManager.default.attributesOfItem(atPath: target) else { return .missing(path: path) }
            guard attributes[.type] as? FileAttributeType == .typeRegular else { return .notAFile(path: path) }
            return .file(path: path, modified: attributes[.modificationDate] as? Date ?? .distantPast,
                         size: (attributes[.size] as? NSNumber)?.intValue ?? 0)
        }
    }

    // MARK: Reading

    /// The bytes of `reference`, never more than `ImageLimits.maxBytes`: a linked file is read at most that far (plus one
    /// byte to notice it is bigger), so a file that grows or lies about its size cannot fill memory.
    public func bytes(of reference: ImageReference) throws(ImageProblem) -> Data {
        switch reference {
        case .embedded(let hash):
            guard let data = embedded[hash] else { throw .embeddedMissing }
            return data
        case .linked:
            let name = reference.fileName ?? "image"
            switch stamp(of: reference) {
            case .missing(let path):
                throw .notFound(name: name, folder: Self.folder(ofPath: path))
            case .notAFile:
                throw .notAFile(name: name)
            case .file(let path, _, let size):
                guard size <= ImageLimits.maxBytes else { throw .tooManyBytes(size) }
                return try Self.read(URL(filePath: path).resolvingSymlinksInPath(), name: name)
            case .embedded:
                throw .embeddedMissing
            }
        }
    }

    /// Reads the file at `url`, at most `ImageLimits.maxBytes`. The path was checked a moment ago by `stamp(of:)`, but a path can
    /// be swapped for something else between that check and this open, so what is opened is checked again: it is opened
    /// without blocking (a named pipe would otherwise hold the caller until someone wrote to it) and its descriptor must
    /// be a regular file, whatever the path was.
    static func read(_ url: URL, name: String) throws(ImageProblem) -> Data {
        let descriptor = open(url.path(percentEncoded: false), O_RDONLY | O_NONBLOCK | O_CLOEXEC)
        guard descriptor >= 0 else { throw .unreadable(name: name, reason: String(cString: strerror(errno)) + ".") }
        let handle = FileHandle(fileDescriptor: descriptor, closeOnDealloc: true)
        defer { try? handle.close() }
        var status = stat()
        guard fstat(descriptor, &status) == 0 else { throw .unreadable(name: name, reason: String(cString: strerror(errno)) + ".") }
        guard status.st_mode & S_IFMT == S_IFREG else { throw .notAFile(name: name) }
        guard status.st_size <= ImageLimits.maxBytes else { throw .tooManyBytes(Int(status.st_size)) }
        let data: Data
        do {
            data = try handle.read(upToCount: ImageLimits.maxBytes + 1) ?? Data()
        } catch {
            throw .unreadable(name: name, reason: error.localizedDescription)
        }
        guard data.count <= ImageLimits.maxBytes else { throw .tooManyBytes(data.count) }
        return data
    }

    /// The bytes of the file at `url` as an image to choose or embed, with the same bounds as `bytes(of:)`.
    public static func bytes(ofFileAt url: URL) throws(ImageProblem) -> Data {
        let resolver = ImageResolver()
        return try resolver.bytes(of: .linked(relativePath: nil, absolutePath: url.path(percentEncoded: false)))
    }

    // MARK: Saving

    /// `reference` as the document saved in `folder` should store it: a linked file that can be found is written with its
    /// absolute path and a path relative to `folder` (an unsaved document gains its relative path on first save); one that
    /// cannot be found is stored as it was, so a missing file keeps both of its paths. An embedded image is unchanged.
    public func stored(_ reference: ImageReference, inDocumentFolder folder: URL?) -> ImageReference {
        guard case .linked = reference, let url = location(of: reference),
              FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: url.path(percentEncoded: false)) else { return reference }
        return .linked(relativePath: folder.map { Self.relativePath(from: $0, to: url) }, absolutePath: url.path(percentEncoded: false))
    }

    /// The path of `file` relative to `folder`, such as `textures/vent.png` or `../shared/vent.png`. Symbolic links are
    /// resolved on both sides first, so `/tmp` and `/private/tmp` agree.
    public static func relativePath(from folder: URL, to file: URL) -> String {
        let from = folder.resolvingSymlinksInPath().standardizedFileURL.pathComponents
        let to = file.resolvingSymlinksInPath().standardizedFileURL.pathComponents
        var shared = 0
        while shared < min(from.count, to.count), from[shared] == to[shared] { shared += 1 }
        let parts = [String](repeating: "..", count: from.count - shared) + to[shared...]
        return parts.joined(separator: "/")
    }

    /// The folder part of a path, written with a closing slash ("/Users/me/textures/").
    static func folder(ofPath path: String) -> String {
        URL(filePath: path).deletingLastPathComponent().path(percentEncoded: false)
    }
}
```

`Sources/CreatorGraph/Images/ImageStamp.swift` (new file)

```swift
import Foundation

/// What an image reference points at right now, cheap to read and to compare. The evaluator mixes it into a node's cache key
/// at every evaluation (fields spec D11), so a linked file edited on disk makes the node run again and an untouched one does not.
public enum ImageStamp: Hashable, Sendable {
    /// An embedded image: whether the document holds its bytes.
    case embedded(ImageHash, isPresent: Bool)
    /// A linked regular file with its modification date and size.
    case file(path: String, modified: Date, size: Int)
    /// A linked path with nothing at it.
    case missing(path: String)
    /// A linked path that is not a regular file.
    case notAFile(path: String)

    /// Whether what this stamp names is a definite thing (an embedded image the document holds, a regular file with its date
    /// and size), so a picture or a failure to decode it may be remembered under it. A missing path or a non-file is not.
    public var isKeepable: Bool {
        switch self {
        case .embedded(_, isPresent: true), .file: true
        case .embedded, .missing, .notAFile: false
        }
    }
}
```

### Task 5: The Image Field node

The node itself, its decoded-picture cache (which also remembers pictures that could not be decoded) and its loader, registered in `BuiltInNodes` after Noise Field. The `image` setting has no inspector control yet (Task 8); a test sets it directly.

**Files:**

- Modify: `Sources/CreatorNodes/BuiltInNodes.swift`
- Create: `Sources/CreatorNodes/Fields/GreyImageCache.swift`
- Create: `Sources/CreatorNodes/Fields/ImageLoader.swift`
- Create: `Sources/CreatorNodes/Fields/Sources/ImageFieldNode.swift`

Tests:

- Modify: `Tests/CreatorEditorTests/FieldsLibraryTests.swift`
- Create: `Tests/CreatorNodesTests/Fields/GreyImageCacheTests.swift`
- Create: `Tests/CreatorNodesTests/Fields/ImageFieldNodeTests.swift`
- Modify: `Tests/CreatorNodesTests/OutputNodeTests.swift`
- Modify: `Tests/CreatorNodesTests/Support/NodeTestSupport.swift`
- Create: `Tests/CreatorNodesTests/Support/TempFolderForNodes.swift`

**Interfaces:**

- Consumes: `ImageResolver`, `ImageLoader` inputs from Task 4; `GreyImageDecoder.decode`; `Field.image`; `NodeDefinition` / `SocketSpec` / `InspectorSection`.
- Produces: `ImageFieldNode` (`creator.imageField`; inputs `plane`, `width`, `height`, `edges`; output `field`); `GreyImageCache` (shared, 256 MB, LRU, keyed by `ImageStamp`; `problem(for:)` / `storeProblem(_:for:)` keep at most 256 failures); `ImageLoader.image(for:resolver:cache:decode:)` (`decode` defaults to `GreyImageDecoder.decode`; a test passes a counting one); `Harness.run(_:kernel:images:)`; test fixture `TempFolderForNodes`.

**Note:** Existing tests that count the catalog change here, and 7b-3 changes the same numbers (see "Files shared with the other track"): `OutputNodeTests` (65 nodes, `.fields: 13`) and `FieldsLibraryTests` ("Image Field" after "Noise Field"). Order of checks in `evaluate`: no picture, then width / height / plane / edges (cheap), then the picture (read and decode), so a bad number never costs a decode.

**A picture that cannot be decoded is remembered, not retried.** A failed node is not cached by the evaluator, so without this a file whose header passes `inspect` but whose body does not (a JPEG cut short, a PNG with a damaged body) would be read and decoded to the point of failure at *every* evaluation, including every slider tick anywhere in the document, which for a large picture is seconds each time. `ImageLoader` stores the `ImageProblem` under the picture's `ImageStamp` (an embedded picture's hash, or a file's path, modification date and size), so an unchanged damaged file fails from memory, and a changed stamp tries it again (`aDamagedFileIsDecodedOnceAndThenFailsAtOnceUntilItChanges`, with a counting decoder). Only failures the *bytes* decide are kept: a file that could not be read (permissions, a volume that went away) fails before any decoding costs anything and is tried again, because fixing it (`chmod`) changes no stamp (`aFileThatCouldNotBeReadIsTriedAgainBecauseFixingThatChangesNoStamp`); a missing path or a non-file has no stamp worth keeping (`ImageStamp.isKeepable`). At most 256 failures are kept, least recently used first.

`Tests/CreatorNodesTests/Fields/GreyImageCacheTests.swift` (new)

```swift
import CreatorGraph
import Foundation
import Synchronization
import Testing
@testable import CreatorNodes

/// `GreyImageCache` and `ImageLoader`: decoded pictures are kept by what they were read from, and evicted least recently used.
struct GreyImageCacheTests {
    func picture(_ pixels: Int, value: UInt16 = 7) throws -> GreyImage {
        try #require(GreyImage(width: pixels, height: 1, pixels: [UInt16](repeating: value, count: pixels)))
    }

    func stamp(_ name: String) -> ImageStamp { .file(path: "/\(name)", modified: Date(timeIntervalSince1970: 1), size: 1) }

    @Test func aStoredPictureComesBackAndAnotherKeyMisses() throws {
        let cache = GreyImageCache(budgetBytes: 1_000)
        try cache.store(picture(10), for: stamp("a"))
        #expect(cache.image(for: stamp("a"))?.width == 10)
        #expect(cache.image(for: stamp("b")) == nil)
        let later = ImageStamp.file(path: "/a", modified: Date(timeIntervalSince1970: 2), size: 1)
        #expect(cache.image(for: later) == nil, "the file was modified: a different key")
    }

    @Test func theLeastRecentlyUsedPictureGoesFirstWhenOverBudget() throws {
        let cache = GreyImageCache(budgetBytes: 100)  // room for five 10-pixel pictures of 20 bytes
        for name in ["a", "b", "c", "d", "e"] { try cache.store(picture(10), for: stamp(name)) }
        #expect(cache.count == 5)
        _ = cache.image(for: stamp("a"))  // a is now the most recently used
        try cache.store(picture(10), for: stamp("f"))
        #expect(cache.count == 5)
        #expect(cache.image(for: stamp("a")) != nil && cache.image(for: stamp("f")) != nil)
        #expect(cache.image(for: stamp("b")) == nil, "b was the oldest")
    }

    @Test func aPictureBiggerThanTheWholeBudgetIsNotKept() throws {
        let cache = GreyImageCache(budgetBytes: 100)
        try cache.store(picture(60), for: stamp("big"))
        #expect(cache.count == 0)
    }

    @Test func theLoaderDecodesOnceAndReusesThePictureUntilTheStampChanges() async throws {
        let folder = TempFolderForNodes()
        let cache = GreyImageCache(budgetBytes: 1_000_000)
        let file = try folder.write(TestImages.flatGrey(4, 4, 100), to: "a.png")
        try folder.touch(file, Date(timeIntervalSince1970: 1_000))
        let reference = ImageReference.linked(relativePath: nil, absolutePath: file.path(percentEncoded: false))
        let resolver = ImageResolver()
        let first = try await ImageLoader.image(for: reference, resolver: resolver, cache: cache)
        #expect(first.pixels.allSatisfy { $0 == 100 * 257 } && cache.count == 1)
        // Replace the bytes but keep size and date: the stamp cannot tell, so the kept picture is used (D11's rule is the date).
        try folder.write(TestImages.flatGrey(4, 4, 200), to: "a.png")
        try folder.touch(file, Date(timeIntervalSince1970: 1_000))
        #expect(try await ImageLoader.image(for: reference, resolver: resolver, cache: cache).pixels[0] == 100 * 257)
        try folder.touch(file, Date(timeIntervalSince1970: 2_000))
        #expect(try await ImageLoader.image(for: reference, resolver: resolver, cache: cache).pixels[0] == 200 * 257)
        #expect(cache.count == 2)
    }

    @Test func aMissingOrBadImageIsAProblemAndNothingIsKept() async throws {
        let cache = GreyImageCache(budgetBytes: 1_000_000)
        let gone = ImageReference.linked(relativePath: nil, absolutePath: "/no/such/dir/x.png")
        await #expect(throws: ImageProblem.notFound(name: "x.png", folder: "/no/such/dir/")) {
            try await ImageLoader.image(for: gone, resolver: ImageResolver(), cache: cache)
        }
        let junk = Data("not a picture".utf8)
        await #expect(throws: ImageProblem.undecodable) {
            let hash = ImageHash(of: junk)
            return try await ImageLoader.image(for: .embedded(hash), resolver: ImageResolver(embedded: [hash: junk]), cache: cache)
        }
        #expect(cache.count == 0)
    }

    // MARK: Pictures that cannot be decoded

    /// A decoder that counts its calls, so a test can see when the loader decodes and when it does not.
    final class CountingDecoder: Sendable {
        private let calls = Mutex(0)
        var count: Int { calls.withLock { $0 } }

        var decode: ImageLoader.Decode {
            { data in
                self.calls.withLock { $0 += 1 }
                return try await GreyImageDecoder.decode(data)
            }
        }
    }

    @Test func aDamagedFileIsDecodedOnceAndThenFailsAtOnceUntilItChanges() async throws {
        let folder = TempFolderForNodes()
        let cache = GreyImageCache(budgetBytes: 1_000_000)
        let counter = CountingDecoder()
        let whole = TestImages.noisy(64, type: .jpeg)
        let file = try folder.write(whole.prefix(whole.count / 2), to: "cut.jpg")
        try folder.touch(file, Date(timeIntervalSince1970: 1_000))
        let reference = ImageReference.linked(relativePath: nil, absolutePath: file.path(percentEncoded: false))
        for _ in 0..<3 {
            await #expect(throws: ImageProblem.undecodable) {
                try await ImageLoader.image(for: reference, resolver: ImageResolver(), cache: cache, decode: counter.decode)
            }
        }
        #expect(counter.count == 1, "the cut-short JPEG was read and decoded once; the next two evaluations failed from memory")
        #expect(cache.problemCount == 1 && cache.count == 0)
        // Saving the whole picture changes the file's date and size, so the stamp changes and the file is tried again.
        try folder.write(whole, to: "cut.jpg")
        try folder.touch(file, Date(timeIntervalSince1970: 2_000))
        let image = try await ImageLoader.image(for: reference, resolver: ImageResolver(), cache: cache, decode: counter.decode)
        #expect(counter.count == 2 && image.width == 64)
    }

    @Test func aDamagedEmbeddedPictureIsRememberedByItsHash() async throws {
        let cache = GreyImageCache(budgetBytes: 1_000_000)
        let counter = CountingDecoder()
        let png = TestImages.noisy(32)
        let damaged = Data(png.prefix(png.count / 2))
        let hash = ImageHash(of: damaged)
        let resolver = ImageResolver(embedded: [hash: damaged])
        for _ in 0..<2 {
            await #expect(throws: ImageProblem.undecodable) {
                try await ImageLoader.image(for: .embedded(hash), resolver: resolver, cache: cache, decode: counter.decode)
            }
        }
        #expect(counter.count == 1)
    }

    @Test func aFileThatCouldNotBeReadIsTriedAgainBecauseFixingThatChangesNoStamp() async throws {
        let folder = TempFolderForNodes()
        let cache = GreyImageCache(budgetBytes: 1_000_000)
        let counter = CountingDecoder()
        let file = try folder.write(TestImages.flatGrey(4, 4, 90), to: "locked.png")
        let path = file.path(percentEncoded: false)
        try FileManager.default.setAttributes([.posixPermissions: 0o000], ofItemAtPath: path)
        defer { try? FileManager.default.setAttributes([.posixPermissions: 0o644], ofItemAtPath: path) }
        let reference = ImageReference.linked(relativePath: nil, absolutePath: path)
        await #expect(throws: ImageProblem.self) {
            try await ImageLoader.image(for: reference, resolver: ImageResolver(), cache: cache, decode: counter.decode)
        }
        #expect(cache.problemCount == 0, "a read error is not remembered")
        try FileManager.default.setAttributes([.posixPermissions: 0o644], ofItemAtPath: path)
        let image = try await ImageLoader.image(for: reference, resolver: ImageResolver(), cache: cache, decode: counter.decode)
        #expect(image.pixels.allSatisfy { $0 == 90 * 257 })
    }

    @Test func theRememberedProblemsAreBoundedAndTheOldestGoFirst() {
        let cache = GreyImageCache(budgetBytes: 1_000)
        for index in 0..<(GreyImageCache.maxProblems + 10) { cache.storeProblem(.undecodable, for: stamp("p\(index)")) }
        #expect(cache.problemCount == GreyImageCache.maxProblems)
        #expect(cache.problem(for: stamp("p0")) == nil && cache.problem(for: stamp("p\(GreyImageCache.maxProblems + 9)")) == .undecodable)
    }
}
```

```swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorKernel
import Foundation
import Testing
@testable import CreatorNodes

/// Image Field (fields spec §3.1, §5) through the real evaluator: the field it makes and every way it refuses.
struct ImageFieldNodeTests {
    let folder = TempFolderForNodes()
    /// 4 × 2 px, 8-bit: the top row 0, 85, 170, 255 and the bottom row its mirror.
    let ramp = TestImages.grey(4, 2) { x, y in y == 0 ? x * 85 : (3 - x) * 85 }

    /// An Image Field with `data` embedded, and the resolver that holds it.
    func embedded(_ data: Data, _ values: [SocketName: ConstantValue] = [:]) -> (Harness, Node, ImageResolver) {
        var h = Harness()
        let hash = ImageHash(of: data)
        let node = h.add(ImageFieldNode.self, values.merging([NodeSetting.image: .image(.embedded(hash))]) { _, new in new })
        return (h, node, ImageResolver(embedded: [hash: data]))
    }

    @Test func itMakesAFieldOfThePicturesGreyCentredOnThePlane() async throws {
        let (h, node, images) = embedded(ramp, ["width": .number(8), "height": .number(4)])
        let report = try await h.run([node], images: images)
        #expect(report.field(node)?.description == "image(4 × 2 px over 8 × 4 mm, tile)")
        let points = [Vector3(-3, 1, 0), Vector3(-1, 1, 0), Vector3(1, 1, 0), Vector3(3, 1, 0), Vector3(-3, -1, 0), Vector3(3, -1, 0)]
        let found = try await sampled(report.field(node), at: points)
        #expect(found == [0, 85.0 / 255, 170.0 / 255, 1, 1, 0])
    }

    @Test func theEdgeSettingChoosesBetweenTilingAndClamping() async throws {
        let outside = [Vector3(5, 1, 0), Vector3(-5, 1, 0)]
        let (tile, tileNode, images) = embedded(ramp, ["width": .number(8), "height": .number(4), "edges": .integer(0)])
        let tiled = try await sampled(tile.run([tileNode], images: images).field(tileNode), at: outside)
        #expect(tiled == [0, 1], "x 5 is a pixel past the right edge, which wraps to column 0; x −5 is a pixel before the left one")
        let (clamp, clampNode, _) = embedded(ramp, ["width": .number(8), "height": .number(4), "edges": .integer(1)])
        let held = try await sampled(clamp.run([clampNode], images: images).field(clampNode), at: outside)
        #expect(held == [1, 0], "the nearest edge pixel on each side")
        #expect(try await clamp.run([clampNode], images: images).field(clampNode)?.description.hasSuffix(", clamp)") == true)
    }

    @Test func itFollowsAWiredPlane() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let data = ramp
        let plane = h.add(PlaneNode.self, ["offset": .number(30)])
        let reference = ConstantValue.image(.embedded(ImageHash(of: data)))
        let node = h.add(ImageFieldNode.self, ["width": .number(8), "height": .number(4), NodeSetting.image: reference])
        h.wire(plane, "plane", to: node, "plane")
        let report = try await h.run([node], images: ImageResolver(embedded: [ImageHash(of: data): data]))
        let found = try await sampled(report.field(node), at: [Vector3(3, 1, 30), Vector3(3, 1, -5)])
        #expect(found == [1, 1], "projected along the normal")
    }

    @Test func aLinkedFileIsReadRelativeToTheDocumentAndAChangeOnDiskIsSeenNextTime() async throws {
        let file = try folder.write(TestImages.flatGrey(2, 2, 51), to: "maps/vent.png")
        try folder.touch(file, Date(timeIntervalSince1970: 1_000))
        var h = Harness()
        let reference = ImageReference.linked(relativePath: "maps/vent.png", absolutePath: "/gone/vent.png")
        let node = h.add(ImageFieldNode.self, [NodeSetting.image: .image(reference)])
        let images = ImageResolver(documentFolder: folder.url)
        #expect(try await sampled(h.run([node], images: images).field(node), at: [Vector3.zero]) == [0.2])
        try folder.write(TestImages.flatGrey(2, 2, 255), to: "maps/vent.png")
        try folder.touch(file, Date(timeIntervalSince1970: 2_000))
        #expect(try await sampled(h.run([node], images: images).field(node), at: [Vector3.zero]) == [1])
    }

    // MARK: Refusals

    @Test func noImageChosenIsAnErrorThatSaysWhatToDo() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let node = h.add(ImageFieldNode.self)
        #expect(try await h.run([node]).error(node) == "Image Field needs an image: choose one in the inspector.")
    }

    @Test func aMissingLinkNamesTheFileAndTheFolderAndAnAbsentEmbeddedImageSaysSo() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let path = folder.url.appending(path: "textures/vent-mask.png").path(percentEncoded: false)
        let linked = h.add(ImageFieldNode.self, [NodeSetting.image: .image(.linked(relativePath: nil, absolutePath: path))])
        let absent = h.add(ImageFieldNode.self, [NodeSetting.image: .image(.embedded(ImageHash(of: Data("x".utf8))))])
        let report = try await h.run([linked, absent])
        let message = try #require(report.error(linked))
        #expect(message.hasPrefix("Image 'vent-mask.png' wasn't found at ") && message.hasSuffix("/textures/. Relink it in the inspector."))
        #expect(report.error(absent) == ImageProblem.embeddedMissing.message)
    }

    @Test func aFileThatIsNotAnImageIsAnErrorNotACrash() async throws {
        let junk = Data("this is not a picture".utf8)
        let (h, node, images) = embedded(junk)
        #expect(try await h.run([node], images: images).error(node) == ImageProblem.undecodable.message)
        let (hg, gif, gifImages) = embedded(TestImages.flatGrey(8, 8, 3, type: .gif))
        #expect(try await hg.run([gif], images: gifImages).error(gif) == ImageProblem.unsupportedType.message)
        let (hw, wide, wideImages) = embedded(TestImages.flatGrey(9_000, 1, 3))
        #expect(try await hw.run([wide], images: wideImages).error(wide) == ImageProblem.tooManyPixels(width: 9_000, height: 1).message)
    }

    @Test func aColourPictureIsUsedAsItsLuminance() async throws {
        let (h, node, images) = embedded(TestImages.colour(2, 2, red: 255, green: 255, blue: 255))
        #expect(try await sampled(h.run([node], images: images).field(node), at: [Vector3.zero]) == [1])
    }

    @Test func aBadSizeEdgeOrPlaneIsRefusedWithItsOwnSentence() async throws {
        let cases: [(SocketName, ConstantValue, String)] = [
            ("width", .number(0), "“width” must be greater than 0 mm."),
            ("height", .number(-3), "“height” must be greater than 0 mm."),
            ("width", .number(.infinity), "“width” must be greater than 0 mm."),
            ("edges", .integer(2), "“edges” must be Tile or Clamp."),
            ("plane", .plane(Plane(origin: .zero, normal: .zero, xAxis: .unitX)), "“plane” has no direction."),
            ("plane", .plane(Plane(origin: .zero, normal: .unitZ, xAxis: .unitZ)), "“plane” has no direction."),
        ]
        for (socket, value, message) in cases {
            let (h, node, images) = embedded(ramp, [socket: value])
            #expect(try await h.run([node], images: images).error(node) == message, "\(socket)")
        }
    }

    @Test func theNodeIsAFieldsSourceWithItsOwnSettingAndASegmentedEdgeControl() throws {
        #expect(ImageFieldNode.category == .fields && ImageFieldNode.outputs.map(\.type) == [.field])
        #expect(ImageFieldNode.defaultSettings.isEmpty, "no picture until one is chosen")
        #expect(BuiltInNodes.registry[ImageFieldNode.typeID] != nil)
    }
}
```

```swift
// Test fixture file: a self-removing folder under the temporary directory, for the node tests that link to real files.
import Foundation

final class TempFolderForNodes {
    let url: URL

    init() {
        url = URL.temporaryDirectory.appending(path: "mc-nodes-\(UUID().uuidString)", directoryHint: .isDirectory)
        try? FileManager.default.createDirectory(at: url, withIntermediateDirectories: true)
    }

    deinit { try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: url) }

    /// Writes `data` at `name` (a path under the folder, its folders made) and returns the file's URL.
    @discardableResult
    func write(_ data: Data, to name: String) throws -> URL {
        let file = url.appending(path: name)
        try FileManager.default.createDirectory(at: file.deletingLastPathComponent(), withIntermediateDirectories: true)
        try data.write(to: file)
        return file
    }

    /// Sets a file's modification date.
    func touch(_ file: URL, _ date: Date) throws {
        try FileManager.default.setAttributes([.modificationDate: date], ofItemAtPath: file.path(percentEncoded: false))
    }
}
```

`Sources/CreatorNodes/Fields/GreyImageCache.swift` (new file)

```swift
import CreatorGraph
import Synchronization

/// Decoded pictures kept by what they were read from (fields spec §5): an embedded image by its content hash, a linked file by
/// path, modification date and size (`ImageStamp`), so editing a linked file misses the cache and a node that is edited
/// downstream never decodes again. Least recently used pictures go first when the pictures kept pass `budgetBytes`.
///
/// A picture that could not be decoded is remembered too (`problem(for:)`), under the same stamp, so a damaged file that is
/// unchanged fails at once at every evaluation instead of being read and decoded again each time; it is retried as soon as
/// its stamp changes. At most `maxProblems` are kept, least recently used first. Shared by every document in the process,
/// because what it stores is a pure function of its key.
final class GreyImageCache: Sendable {
    /// How many failed pictures are remembered.
    static let maxProblems = 256

    /// The cache the Image Field node uses: 256 MB, room for two of the largest legal pictures.
    static let shared = GreyImageCache(budgetBytes: 256 * 1024 * 1024)

    private struct State {
        var entries: [ImageStamp: (image: GreyImage, lastUsed: Int)] = [:]
        var problems: [ImageStamp: (problem: ImageProblem, lastUsed: Int)] = [:]
        var clock = 0
    }

    let budgetBytes: Int
    private let state = Mutex(State())

    init(budgetBytes: Int) {
        self.budgetBytes = budgetBytes
    }

    /// The kept picture for `stamp`, marked as just used.
    func image(for stamp: ImageStamp) -> GreyImage? {
        state.withLock { state in
            guard let entry = state.entries[stamp] else { return nil }
            state.clock += 1
            state.entries[stamp] = (entry.image, state.clock)
            return entry.image
        }
    }

    /// Keeps `image` for `stamp`, dropping the least recently used pictures while the total is over budget. A picture bigger
    /// than the whole budget is not kept.
    func store(_ image: GreyImage, for stamp: ImageStamp) {
        guard image.byteCount <= budgetBytes else { return }
        state.withLock { state in
            state.clock += 1
            state.entries[stamp] = (image, state.clock)
            var total = state.entries.values.reduce(0) { $0 + $1.image.byteCount }
            while total > budgetBytes, let oldest = state.entries.min(by: { $0.value.lastUsed < $1.value.lastUsed }) {
                total -= oldest.value.image.byteCount
                state.entries[oldest.key] = nil
            }
        }
    }

    /// Why the picture for `stamp` could not be decoded, if that was found out before; marked as just used.
    func problem(for stamp: ImageStamp) -> ImageProblem? {
        state.withLock { state in
            guard let entry = state.problems[stamp] else { return nil }
            state.clock += 1
            state.problems[stamp] = (entry.problem, state.clock)
            return entry.problem
        }
    }

    /// Remembers that the picture for `stamp` is unusable, dropping the least recently used problems past `maxProblems`.
    func storeProblem(_ problem: ImageProblem, for stamp: ImageStamp) {
        state.withLock { state in
            state.clock += 1
            state.problems[stamp] = (problem, state.clock)
            while state.problems.count > Self.maxProblems,
                  let oldest = state.problems.min(by: { $0.value.lastUsed < $1.value.lastUsed }) {
                state.problems[oldest.key] = nil
            }
        }
    }

    /// How many failed pictures are remembered.
    var problemCount: Int { state.withLock { $0.problems.count } }

    /// How many pictures are kept.
    var count: Int { state.withLock { $0.entries.count } }
}
```

`Sources/CreatorNodes/Fields/ImageLoader.swift` (new file)

```swift
import CreatorGraph
import Foundation

/// Turns an `ImageReference` into a `GreyImage` for a node (fields spec §5): the picture kept in `GreyImageCache` when the
/// reference still points at what it did, else the bytes read through the evaluation's `ImageResolver` and decoded off the
/// calling actor by `GreyImageDecoder`.
///
/// A file that cannot be decoded (cut short, damaged, not PNG or JPEG) is remembered under its stamp and fails again at
/// once, without being read, until the stamp changes. Only what the bytes decide is remembered: a file that could not be
/// read (permissions, a network volume) is tried again at every evaluation, because fixing that changes no stamp.
enum ImageLoader {
    /// Decodes picture bytes; the default is `GreyImageDecoder.decode`, and a test passes one that counts its calls.
    typealias Decode = @Sendable (Data) async throws -> GreyImage

    static func image(for reference: ImageReference, resolver: ImageResolver, cache: GreyImageCache = .shared,
                      decode: Decode = { try await GreyImageDecoder.decode($0) }) async throws -> GreyImage {
        let stamp = resolver.stamp(of: reference)
        if let kept = cache.image(for: stamp) { return kept }
        if let known = cache.problem(for: stamp) { throw known }
        let data = try resolver.bytes(of: reference)
        let image: GreyImage
        do {
            image = try await decode(data)
        } catch let problem as ImageProblem {
            if stamp.isKeepable { cache.storeProblem(problem, for: stamp) }
            throw problem
        }
        if stamp.isKeepable { cache.store(image, for: stamp) }
        return image
    }
}
```

`Sources/CreatorNodes/Fields/Sources/ImageFieldNode.swift` (new file)

```swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorKernel

/// A picture as a field (fields spec §3.1, §5): the image is projected along the plane's normal, centred on the plane's origin
/// and stretched over `width` × `height` millimetres, and a point's value is the picture's grey there, 0 black to 1 white,
/// bilinear between pixels. Row 0 of the picture is at the top (the plane's +y). Outside the rectangle the picture repeats
/// (Tile) or holds its edge pixels (Clamp). The picture is the node's `NodeSetting.image` setting, embedded or linked.
public enum ImageFieldNode: NodeDefinition {
    public static let typeID = "creator.imageField"
    public static let displayName = "Image Field"
    public static let category = NodeCategory.fields
    public static let inputs = [
        SocketSpec("plane", .plane, defaultValue: .plane(.xy)),
        SocketSpec("width", .number, defaultValue: .number(100), unit: .millimetres, range: 0.1...1000),
        SocketSpec("height", .number, defaultValue: .number(100), unit: .millimetres, range: 0.1...1000),
        SocketSpec("edges", .integer, defaultValue: .integer(ImageEdges.tile.rawValue)),
    ]
    public static let outputs = [SocketSpec("field", .field)]
    public static let inspector = [
        InspectorSection(title: "Projection", controls: [
            .planePicker("plane"), .slider("width"), .slider("height"), .segmented("edges", options: ImageEdges.options),
        ]),
    ]

    public static func evaluate(_ inputs: NodeInputs, kernel: any Kernel, context: EvalContext) async throws -> NodeOutputs {
        guard case .image(let reference)? = context.node.inputValues[NodeSetting.image] else { throw ImageProblem.notChosen }
        let width = try inputs.number("width"), height = try inputs.number("height")
        guard width.isFinite, width > 0 else { throw NodeError.invalidValue("“width” must be greater than 0 mm.") }
        guard height.isFinite, height > 0 else { throw NodeError.invalidValue("“height” must be greater than 0 mm.") }
        let plane = try inputs.plane("plane")
        guard plane.origin.isFinite, let normal = plane.normal.normalized, let across = plane.xAxis.normalized,
              normal.cross(across).normalized != nil else { throw NodeError.invalidValue("“plane” has no direction.") }
        guard let edges = ImageEdges(rawValue: try inputs.integer("edges")) else {
            throw NodeError.invalidValue("“edges” must be Tile or Clamp.")
        }
        let image = try await ImageLoader.image(for: reference, resolver: context.images)
        let unit = Plane(origin: plane.origin, normal: normal, xAxis: across)
        return NodeOutputs(["field": .field(.image(image, plane: unit, width: width, height: height, edges: edges))])
    }
}
```

### Task 6: File format 7: the `images` table, saving and migration

`GraphFile.currentFormatVersion` becomes 7. The `images` table is read forgivingly and written sorted, only for pictures a node uses; `DocumentModel.fileData(savingTo:)` rewrites linked paths for the folder being saved to, and `DocumentModel.didSave(to:)` brings the open document's own paths into line with them. Formats 1 to 6 open unchanged (there is nothing to transform: format 7 only *adds* a setting kind, a table and a socket type) and are written as 7. Nine existing tests pin "format 6" in the file's text and are updated.

**One bad entry costs one picture.** The table is read as `[String: ImageTable.Entry]`, where an `Entry` is a value that decodes to its string or, for anything that is not a string (a number, `null`, a list, an object), to `nil`; `ImageTable.decode` then drops each entry that is not a good one, alone (`anEntryThatIsNotEvenAStringCostsThatPictureAndNoOtherOne`). Only a table that is not an object at all is ignored whole. This is user decision U2: the document opens, the node whose picture was dropped says so; the dropped entry is **gone from the file for good once the person saves** (`savingAfterADamagedEntryWasLeftOutWritesTheFileWithoutIt`), because a save writes the table from the pictures the nodes use. Keeping the raw entry round-tripping through the session was considered and rejected: it would carry bytes that cannot be used (or a hash that does not match) into every later file. Offering a banner on open (U2's option c) needs a notice channel that does not exist yet.

**Save As follows the document.** `didSave(to:)` runs after the file is written (`AppModel.save(to:)`, Task 9): the open document's linked paths become the ones just written (the relative path made from the new folder), as a change that is no undo step and no edit, and `documentURL` moves. Without it the relative path from the old folder stays, is tried first, and a same-named picture in the new folder silently wins until the next reopen. (An undo past an earlier relink can still bring back that step's own old paths; the edit is the person's, and the file on disk is not touched by it.)

**Files:**

- Modify: `Sources/CreatorGraph/DocumentModel.swift`
- Modify: `Sources/CreatorGraph/GraphFile.swift`
- Create: `Sources/CreatorGraph/Images/Graph+Images.swift`
- Create: `Sources/CreatorGraph/Images/ImageTable.swift`

Tests:

- Modify: `Tests/CreatorGraphTests/Comments/CommentFileTests.swift`
- Modify: `Tests/CreatorGraphTests/EdgePickFileTests.swift`
- Modify: `Tests/CreatorGraphTests/Groups/GroupFieldTests.swift`
- Modify: `Tests/CreatorGraphTests/Groups/GroupFileTests.swift`
- Create: `Tests/CreatorGraphTests/Images/ImageFileTests.swift`

- Modify: `Tests/CreatorNodesTests/PatternsAcceptanceTests.swift`
- Modify: `Tests/CreatorNodesTests/Variation/VariationFileTests.swift`

**Interfaces:**

- Consumes: `GraphFile` and `GraphFileIO`; `ImageResolver.stored(_:inDocumentFolder:)`; `DocumentModel.images`, `.documentURL`.
- Produces: `GraphFile.currentFormatVersion == 7`; `ImageTable.encode` / `.decode` (with `ImageTable.Entry`); `Graph.mappingImages(_:)`, `Graph.embeddedImageHashes`; `DocumentModel.fileData(savingTo: URL? = nil)`, `DocumentModel.didSave(to:)`.

**Note:** Two tests marked a saved file "format 5" by replacing the text `"formatVersion" : 6` with `5` to prove an older file opens; with 7 written, that replacement would silently do nothing and the test would stop testing format 5, so both now replace `7`. The `GroupFieldTests` edit here is only its version pin; Task 7 rewrites the file. `GraphFile.init(from:)` reads the `images` table with `try?`, so a value of the wrong shape (an array, a number) is ignored rather than failing the whole decode.
