`Tests/CreatorGraphTests/Images/ImageFileTests.swift` (new)

```swift
import CreatorKernel
import Foundation
import Testing
@testable import CreatorGraph

/// File format 7 (fields spec §5): the `images` table, the `image` setting, what a save keeps, and formats 1 to 6.
@MainActor
struct ImageFileTests {
    static let registry = NodeRegistry([ImageProbeNode.self])
    let folder = TempFolder()
    let first = Data("first picture".utf8)
    let second = Data("second picture, longer".utf8)

    func probe(_ reference: ImageReference) -> Node {
        var node = Self.registry.makeNode(ImageProbeNode.typeID)
        node.inputValues[NodeSetting.image] = .image(reference)
        return node
    }

    func document(_ nodes: [Node], images: [Data] = []) -> DocumentModel {
        let table = Dictionary(uniqueKeysWithValues: images.map { (ImageHash(of: $0), $0) })
        let file = GraphFile(graph: Graph(nodes: Dictionary(uniqueKeysWithValues: nodes.map { ($0.id, $0) })), images: table)
        return DocumentModel(file: file, registry: Self.registry, kernel: FakeKernel())
    }

    func json(_ data: Data) throws -> [String: Any] {
        try #require(try JSONSerialization.jsonObject(with: data) as? [String: Any])
    }

    func reopened(_ data: Data) throws -> GraphFile { try GraphFileIO.decode(data, registry: Self.registry) }

    // MARK: Format 7

    @Test func filesAreWrittenAsFormatSevenAndAnImageFreeFileHasNoImagesKey() throws {
        #expect(GraphFile.currentFormatVersion == 7)
        let object = try json(try document([]).fileData())
        #expect(object["formatVersion"] as? Int == 7)
        #expect(object["images"] == nil, "written only when there are images")
    }

    @Test func embeddedImagesRoundTripThroughTheFileKeyedByHashAndSorted() throws {
        let nodes = [probe(.embedded(ImageHash(of: first))), probe(.embedded(ImageHash(of: second)))]
        let data = try document(nodes, images: [first, second]).fileData()
        let table = try #require(try json(data)["images"] as? [String: String])
        #expect(table.count == 2 && table[ImageHash(of: first).hex] == first.base64EncodedString())
        let text = try #require(String(bytes: data, encoding: .utf8))
        let positions = table.keys.sorted().compactMap { text.range(of: "\"\($0)\" :")?.lowerBound }
        #expect(positions.count == 2 && positions == positions.sorted(), "written sorted by key, so a save changes only what changed")
        let file = try reopened(data)
        #expect(file.images == [ImageHash(of: first): first, ImageHash(of: second): second])
        #expect(try GraphFileIO.encode(file) == GraphFileIO.encode(GraphFile(graph: file.graph, images: file.images)))
    }

    @Test func twoNodesWithOnePictureStoreItOnce() throws {
        let hash = ImageHash(of: first)
        let data = try document([probe(.embedded(hash)), probe(.embedded(hash))], images: [first]).fileData()
        #expect(try #require(try json(data)["images"] as? [String: String]).count == 1)
    }

    @Test func imagesNoNodeUsesAreDroppedOnSaveButKeptForTheSession() throws {
        let document = document([probe(.embedded(ImageHash(of: first)))], images: [first, second])
        let object = try json(try document.fileData())
        #expect(Set(try #require(object["images"] as? [String: String]).keys) == [ImageHash(of: first).hex])
        #expect(document.images.count == 2, "undo may still want the other")
    }

    @Test func aLinkedImageStoresNoBytes() throws {
        let file = try folder.write(first, to: "vent.png")
        let reference = ImageReference.linked(relativePath: nil, absolutePath: file.path(percentEncoded: false))
        let document = document([probe(reference)], images: [second])
        #expect(try json(try document.fileData())["images"] == nil)
    }

    @Test func imagesUsedInsideAGroupDefinitionAreKept() throws {
        let inner = probe(.embedded(ImageHash(of: second)))
        let definition = GroupDefinition(id: GroupID(), name: "Inside", inputs: [], outputs: [], graph: Graph(nodes: [inner.id: inner]))
        let file = GraphFile(definitions: [definition.id: definition], images: [ImageHash(of: second): second])
        let document = DocumentModel(file: file, registry: Self.registry, kernel: FakeKernel())
        #expect(try #require(try json(try document.fileData())["images"] as? [String: String]).count == 1)
    }

    @Test func aDamagedEntryIsLeftOutAndTheRestOfTheDocumentOpens() throws {
        let good = ImageHash(of: first)
        let table: [String: String] = [
            good.hex: first.base64EncodedString(),
            ImageHash(of: second).hex: "###not base64###",
            String(repeating: "a", count: 64): second.base64EncodedString(),  // bytes that do not hash to the key
            "not-a-hash": first.base64EncodedString(),
            String(repeating: "B", count: 64): first.base64EncodedString(),  // upper case is no hash
        ]
        var object = try json(try document([]).fileData())
        object["images"] = table
        let file = try reopened(try JSONSerialization.data(withJSONObject: object))
        #expect(file.images == [good: first])
    }

    @Test func aPictureOverSixteenMegabytesInATableIsLeftOut() throws {
        let big = Data(count: ImageLimits.maxBytes + 1)
        var object = try json(try document([]).fileData())
        object["images"] = [ImageHash(of: big).hex: big.base64EncodedString()]
        #expect(try reopened(try JSONSerialization.data(withJSONObject: object)).images.isEmpty)
    }

    @Test func anImagesKeyOfTheWrongShapeIsIgnoredNotFatal() throws {
        var object = try json(try document([probe(.embedded(ImageHash(of: first)))]).fileData())
        object["images"] = ["a", "b"]
        let file = try reopened(try JSONSerialization.data(withJSONObject: object))
        #expect(file.images.isEmpty && file.graph.nodes.count == 1)
    }

    @Test func anEntryThatIsNotEvenAStringCostsThatPictureAndNoOtherOne() throws {
        let good = ImageHash(of: first)
        var object = try json(try document([]).fileData())
        object["images"] = [
            good.hex: first.base64EncodedString(),
            ImageHash(of: second).hex: 12,
            String(repeating: "c", count: 64): NSNull(),
            String(repeating: "d", count: 64): ["not", "a", "string"],
            String(repeating: "e", count: 64): ["nested": true],
            String(repeating: "f", count: 64): true,
        ] as [String: Any]
        let file = try reopened(try JSONSerialization.data(withJSONObject: object))
        #expect(file.images == [good: first], "the one good picture survives its damaged neighbours")
    }

    @Test func savingAfterADamagedEntryWasLeftOutWritesTheFileWithoutIt() throws {
        let good = ImageHash(of: first), bad = ImageHash(of: second)
        var object = try json(try document([]).fileData())
        object["images"] = [good.hex: first.base64EncodedString(), bad.hex: 12] as [String: Any]
        let file = try reopened(try JSONSerialization.data(withJSONObject: object))
        let node = probe(.embedded(good))
        let reopened = DocumentModel(file: GraphFile(graph: Graph(nodes: [node.id: node]), images: file.images),
                                     registry: Self.registry, kernel: FakeKernel())
        let table = try #require(try json(try reopened.fileData())["images"] as? [String: String])
        #expect(Set(table.keys) == [good.hex], "the damaged entry is gone from the file for good once it is saved")
    }

    @Test func aDocumentOpenedFromDataHoldsItsImagesAndEvaluatesThem() async throws {
        let data = try document([probe(.embedded(ImageHash(of: first)))], images: [first]).fileData()
        let opened = try DocumentModel(data: data, registry: Self.registry, kernel: FakeKernel())
        #expect(opened.images == [ImageHash(of: first): first])
    }

    // MARK: Linked paths on save

    @Test func anUnsavedDocumentGainsItsRelativePathOnFirstSave() throws {
        let file = try folder.write(first, to: "doc/maps/vent.png")
        let absolute = file.standardizedFileURL.path(percentEncoded: false)
        let document = document([probe(.linked(relativePath: nil, absolutePath: absolute))])
        let destination = folder.url.appending(path: "doc/Plate.mcgraph")
        let saved = try reopened(try document.fileData(savingTo: destination))
        let setting = try #require(saved.graph.nodes.values.first?.inputValues[NodeSetting.image])
        #expect(setting == .image(.linked(relativePath: "maps/vent.png", absolutePath: absolute)))
        let unchanged = try reopened(try document.fileData())
        let kept = unchanged.graph.nodes.values.first?.inputValues[NodeSetting.image]
        #expect(kept == .image(.linked(relativePath: nil, absolutePath: absolute)), "without a destination nothing is rewritten")
    }

    @Test func saveAsElsewhereMakesTheRelativePathFromTheNewFolderAndAMissingFileKeepsItsPaths() throws {
        let file = try folder.write(first, to: "doc/maps/vent.png")
        let absolute = file.standardizedFileURL.path(percentEncoded: false)
        let found = probe(.linked(relativePath: "maps/vent.png", absolutePath: absolute))
        let lost = probe(.linked(relativePath: "gone/x.png", absolutePath: "/no/such/x.png"))
        let document = document([found, lost])
        document.documentURL = folder.url.appending(path: "doc/Plate.mcgraph")
        let saved = try reopened(try document.fileData(savingTo: folder.url.appending(path: "copy/Plate.mcgraph")))
        #expect(saved.graph.nodes[found.id]?.inputValues[NodeSetting.image]
            == .image(.linked(relativePath: "../doc/maps/vent.png", absolutePath: absolute)))
        #expect(saved.graph.nodes[lost.id]?.inputValues[NodeSetting.image] == lost.inputValues[NodeSetting.image])
    }

    @Test func savingWritesNothingBesideALinkedImage() throws {
        let file = try folder.write(first, to: "doc/maps/vent.png")
        let before = folder.listing
        let document = document([probe(.linked(relativePath: nil, absolutePath: file.path(percentEncoded: false)))])
        _ = try document.fileData(savingTo: folder.url.appending(path: "doc/Plate.mcgraph"))
        #expect(folder.listing == before)
    }

    // MARK: Formats 1 to 6 and newer ones

    /// A format-6 file as 7a wrote it: tree nodes' text settings, a comment, no `images`.
    static let formatSix = """
    {
      "definitions" : [],
      "formatVersion" : 6,
      "graph" : {
        "links" : [],
        "nodes" : [
          { "id" : "6C8F1D0C-0000-4000-8000-000000000001", "inputValues" : { "pathRule" : { "type" : "text", "value" : "{A;B} → {B;A}" } },
            "isOutput" : false, "name" : "Path Mapper", "position" : { "x" : 10, "y" : 20 },
            "typeID" : "test.imageProbe", "typeVersion" : 1 }
        ],
        "parameters" : []
      },
      "viewState" : {}
    }
    """

    @Test func aFormatSixFileOpensUnchangedAndIsSavedAsSeven() throws {
        let file = try reopened(Data(Self.formatSix.utf8))
        #expect(file.formatVersion == 6 && file.images.isEmpty && file.graph.nodes.count == 1)
        let document = DocumentModel(file: file, registry: Self.registry, kernel: FakeKernel())
        let saved = try reopened(try document.fileData())
        #expect(saved.formatVersion == 7)
        #expect(saved.graph == file.graph && saved.definitions == file.definitions, "nothing in a format-6 file changes")
    }

    @Test func everyEarlierFormatStillOpens() throws {
        for version in 1...6 {
            let text = Self.formatSix.replacing("\"formatVersion\" : 6", with: "\"formatVersion\" : \(version)")
            #expect(try reopened(Data(text.utf8)).formatVersion == version, "format \(version)")
        }
    }

    @Test func aNewerFormatIsRefusedWithTheExistingMessage() throws {
        let text = Self.formatSix.replacing("\"formatVersion\" : 6", with: "\"formatVersion\" : 8")
        #expect(throws: GraphFileError.newerFormat(8)) { try reopened(Data(text.utf8)) }
        #expect(GraphFileError.newerFormat(8).message.hasPrefix("This file was saved by a newer version of MetalCreator (file format 8)."))
    }
}
```

```diff
+    /// The content with every linked image's paths as they will be found from `folder`, resolving each file from where the
+    /// document is now: a file that can be found gets its absolute path and a path relative to `folder`, so a first save gains
+    /// the relative path and Save As elsewhere makes it again; one that cannot be found keeps the paths it had.
+    private func contentStoringImages(relativeTo folder: URL?) -> GraphContent {
+        let resolver = ImageResolver(embedded: images, documentFolder: documentURL?.deletingLastPathComponent())
+        let store = { (reference: ImageReference) in resolver.stored(reference, inDocumentFolder: folder) }
+        var stored = content
+        stored.graph = content.graph.mappingImages(store)
+        for (id, definition) in content.definitions { stored.definitions[id]?.graph = definition.graph.mappingImages(store) }
+        return stored
     }
 
+    /// The document as a file (always the current format). Only the embedded images a node uses are written, each once; a
+    /// linked image's paths are written as they will be found from `destination`, the file about to be written (else from
+    /// `documentURL`). The open document is not changed (`didSave(to:)` does that once the file is written).
+    public func fileData(savingTo destination: URL? = nil) throws -> Data {
+        let stored = contentStoringImages(relativeTo: (destination ?? documentURL)?.deletingLastPathComponent())
+        let used = stored.definitions.values.reduce(stored.graph.embeddedImageHashes) { $0.union($1.graph.embeddedImageHashes) }
+        let file = GraphFile(graph: stored.graph, definitions: stored.definitions, viewState: viewState,
+                             images: images.filter { used.contains($0.key) })
+        return try GraphFileIO.encode(file)
+    }
+
+    /// Makes `url` the document's file after it was written, and brings the open document's linked images into line with what was
+    /// saved: their paths become the ones `fileData(savingTo:)` wrote, as a change that is no undo step and no edit. Without
+    /// this, a Save As into another folder would keep the old relative paths, which are tried first, so a same-named
+    /// picture in the new folder would silently replace the linked one until the file was reopened. A caller that tracks
+    /// what is saved (`AppModel.savedContent`) reads `content` again afterwards.
+    public func didSave(to url: URL) {
+        let stored = contentStoringImages(relativeTo: url.deletingLastPathComponent())
+        if stored != content { content = stored }
+        documentURL = url
+    }
+
```

`Sources/CreatorGraph/Images/Graph+Images.swift` (new file)

```swift
extension Graph {
    /// This graph with every `image` setting replaced by `transform` of it.
    func mappingImages(_ transform: (ImageReference) -> ImageReference) -> Graph {
        var graph = self
        for (id, node) in nodes {
            for (name, value) in node.inputValues {
                guard case .image(let reference) = value else { continue }
                graph.nodes[id]?.inputValues[name] = .image(transform(reference))
            }
        }
        return graph
    }

    /// The hash of every embedded image a node of this graph points at.
    var embeddedImageHashes: Set<ImageHash> {
        var hashes: Set<ImageHash> = []
        for node in nodes.values {
            for value in node.inputValues.values {
                if case .image(.embedded(let hash)) = value { hashes.insert(hash) }
            }
        }
        return hashes
    }
}
```

`Sources/CreatorGraph/Images/ImageTable.swift` (new file)

```swift
import Foundation

/// The document's `images` table as it is written (fields spec §5): the file's own PNG or JPEG bytes in base64, keyed by the
/// SHA-256 of the bytes. Reading is forgiving, because a damaged picture must not cost the person the rest of the document:
/// an entry whose key is not a hash, whose text is not base64, whose bytes pass 16 MB or do not hash to their key is left out,
/// and a node that points at it says its image is missing. Each entry is judged alone: a value that is not even a string
/// (a number, a list) costs that picture and no other. A save writes the table again from the pictures the nodes use, so a
/// damaged entry that was left out is gone from the file for good once the person saves.
enum ImageTable {
    /// One value of the table as it is read: its text, or `nil` when the value is not a string.
    struct Entry: Decodable {
        let text: String?

        init(from decoder: any Decoder) {
            text = try? decoder.singleValueContainer().decode(String.self)
        }
    }

    static func encode(_ images: [ImageHash: Data]) -> [String: String] {
        Dictionary(uniqueKeysWithValues: images.map { ($0.key.hex, $0.value.base64EncodedString()) })
    }

    static func decode(_ table: [String: Entry]) -> [ImageHash: Data] {
        var images: [ImageHash: Data] = [:]
        for (key, entry) in table {
            guard let text = entry.text, let hash = ImageHash(hex: key), text.utf8.count <= ImageLimits.maxBytes * 4 / 3 + 4,
                  let data = Data(base64Encoded: text), data.count <= ImageLimits.maxBytes,
                  ImageHash(of: data) == hash else { continue }
            images[hash] = data
        }
        return images
    }
}
```

### Task 7: Fields across a group's edge (lifting `GroupRefusal.fieldSocket`)

Format 7 can carry `"type": "field"` in a group interface, so 7b-1's refusal goes. The group tests that pinned the refusal are rewritten to pin the behaviour: a field wire crossing the edge in either direction, a field interface socket with a number default, the file written as 7 and reopened, undo.

**Files:**

- Modify: `Sources/CreatorGraph/Groups/GraphContent+Apply.swift`
- Modify: `Sources/CreatorGraph/Groups/GroupRefusal.swift`

Tests:

- Modify: `Tests/CreatorGraphTests/Groups/GroupFieldTests.swift`

**Interfaces:**

- Consumes: `GraphContent.checkInterface`, `GroupRefusal`, `GroupCommands.group`, `FieldSourceNode` / `FieldSinkNode` (FieldValueTests fixtures).
- Produces: no new API; `GroupRefusal.fieldSocket` is deleted.

**Note:** Nothing else needed to change: Group Input / Group Output pass values through unchanged, `Value.converted(to: .field)` already gives a number default its field, and `SocketSpec`'s coding already had the `field` type name.

### Task 8: The inspector's image rows and the clipboard

`InspectorControl.image(_)` builds a status line ("No image chosen", "Embedded · a1b2c3d4", "Linked · vent-mask.png") and only the buttons that fit; the four `InspectorAction`s reach the app shell as requests; copying nodes carries the embedded pictures they (and their groups' definitions) use; the four undo names exist. `AppModel` ignores the new actions until Task 9 (a `break` keeps the exhaustive switch compiling).

- Produces: `InspectorControl.image(SocketName)`; `InspectorAction.chooseImage`, `.linkImage`, `.relinkImage`, `.embedImage`; `InspectorBuilder.imageRows`; `NodeClipboard.images`; `UndoName.chooseImage`, `.relinkImage`, `.embedImage`, `.linkImage`; `Graph.embeddedImageHashes` made public; the Image Field's "Image" inspector section (first).

**Note:** The buttons are plain `.button` rows, so no new view code is needed and the existing inspector rendering covers them. An embedded picture is named by the start of its hash because it has no file name; showing its pixel size would need the decoded picture in the inspector, which is deliberately not wired (the inspector is data).

`Tests/CreatorEditorTests/ImageInspectorTests.swift` (new)

```swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorKernel
import CreatorNodes
import Foundation
import Testing
@testable import CreatorEditor

/// The Image Field in the inspector and on the clipboard (fields spec §5): what the picture is, the buttons that fit it, and
/// the pictures a copy carries.
@MainActor
struct ImageInspectorTests {
    let registry = BuiltInNodes.registry
    let picture = Data("a picture".utf8)

    func imageField(_ reference: ImageReference?) -> Node {
        var node = registry.makeNode(ImageFieldNode.typeID)
        if let reference { node.inputValues[NodeSetting.image] = .image(reference) }
        return node
    }

    func imageRows(_ node: Node) -> [InspectorRow] {
        let editor = makeEditor([node], registry: registry)
        editor.selection = [node.id]
        return editor.inspectorPage.sections.first { $0.title == "Image" }?.rows ?? []
    }

    @Test func noPictureOffersToChooseOrLinkOne() {
        #expect(imageRows(imageField(nil)) == [
            .readOnly(label: "Image", text: "No image chosen"),
            .button(title: "Choose Image…", action: .chooseImage), .button(title: "Link to File…", action: .linkImage),
        ])
    }

    @Test func anEmbeddedPictureIsNamedByItsHashAndOffersToChooseAnotherOrLinkIt() {
        let hash = ImageHash(of: picture)
        #expect(imageRows(imageField(.embedded(hash))) == [
            .readOnly(label: "Image", text: "Embedded · \(hash.short)"),
            .button(title: "Choose Image…", action: .chooseImage), .button(title: "Link Image…", action: .linkImage),
        ])
    }

    @Test func aLinkedPictureIsNamedByItsFileAndOffersToRelinkOrEmbedIt() {
        let reference = ImageReference.linked(relativePath: "maps/vent-mask.png", absolutePath: "/x/maps/vent-mask.png")
        #expect(imageRows(imageField(reference)) == [
            .readOnly(label: "Image", text: "Linked · vent-mask.png"),
            .button(title: "Relink…", action: .relinkImage), .button(title: "Embed Image", action: .embedImage),
        ])
    }

    @Test func theImageSectionComesFirstAndProjectionFollows() {
        let node = imageField(nil)
        let editor = makeEditor([node], registry: registry)
        editor.selection = [node.id]
        #expect(editor.inspectorPage.sections.map(\.title).prefix(2) == ["Image", "Projection"])
    }

    @Test func eachImageButtonIsHandedToTheAppShellAsARequest() {
        let node = imageField(nil)
        let editor = makeEditor([node], registry: registry)
        for action in [InspectorAction.chooseImage, .linkImage, .relinkImage, .embedImage] {
            let before = editor.inspectorRequest?.serial ?? 0
            editor.press(action, on: node.id)
            #expect(editor.inspectorRequest == InspectorRequest(node: node.id, action: action, serial: before + 1))
        }
    }

    @Test func undoNamesAreTheSpecs() {

    // MARK: Clipboard

    @Test func copyingAnImageFieldCarriesItsPictureAndAPasteAnywhereFindsIt() throws {
        let hash = ImageHash(of: picture)
        let node = imageField(.embedded(hash))
        let source = makeEditor([node], registry: registry)
        source.document.addImage(picture)
        source.document.addImage(Data("an unused picture".utf8))
        source.selection = [node.id]
        source.copySelection()
        #expect(source.clipboard?.images == [hash: picture], "only what the copied nodes use")

        let other = makeEditor([], registry: registry)
        #expect(other.document.images.isEmpty)
        other.setClipboard(try #require(source.clipboard))
        other.paste()
        #expect(other.document.images == [hash: picture])
        let copy = try #require(other.selection.first)
        #expect(other.graph.nodes[copy]?.inputValues[NodeSetting.image] == .image(.embedded(hash)))
    }

    @Test func aLinkedPictureTravelsAsItsPathAlone() throws {
        let node = imageField(.linked(relativePath: nil, absolutePath: "/x/y.png"))
        let editor = makeEditor([node], registry: registry)
        editor.selection = [node.id]
        editor.copySelection()
        #expect(try #require(editor.clipboard).images.isEmpty)
    }

    @Test func aGroupsCopyCarriesThePicturesInsideItsDefinition() throws {
        let hash = ImageHash(of: picture)
        let inner = imageField(.embedded(hash))
        let definition = GroupDefinition(id: GroupID(), name: "Vents", inputs: [], outputs: [], graph: Graph(nodes: [inner.id: inner]))
        var group = registry.withGroups([definition.id: definition]).makeGroupNode(GroupNodes.groupTypeID, for: definition.id)
        group.position = .zero
        let document = DocumentModel(file: GraphFile(graph: Graph(nodes: [group.id: group]), definitions: [definition.id: definition]),
                                     registry: registry, kernel: FakeKernel())
        document.addImage(picture)
        let editor = EditorModel(document: document)
        editor.selection = [group.id]
        editor.copySelection()
        #expect(editor.clipboard?.images == [hash: picture])
    }
}
```

### Task 9: The app shell: choose, link, relink and embed through the file panels

`AppModel.performImage` carries out the four buttons over the window's `FilePicker`: choose (embed), link (an embedded picture is saved where asked, then linked; with none, an existing file is linked), relink, embed. A file is checked when it is chosen, a refusal leaves the setting alone, every success is one undo step with the spec's name. The document's URL now follows New, Open and Save so relative links resolve.

**Files:**

- Modify: `Sources/CreatorApp/AppModel+Files.swift`
- Create: `Sources/CreatorApp/AppModel+Images.swift`
- Modify: `Sources/CreatorApp/AppModel+Picking.swift`
- Modify: `Sources/CreatorApp/AppModel.swift`
- Create: `Sources/CreatorApp/ContentType+Images.swift`
- Modify: `Sources/CreatorApp/DocumentParts.swift`
- Modify: `Sources/CreatorGraph/DocumentModel.swift`

Tests:

- Create: `Tests/CreatorAppTests/ImageActionTests.swift`
- Create: `Tests/CreatorAppTests/Support/AppTempFolder.swift`
- Create: `Tests/CreatorAppTests/Support/AppTestImages.swift`

**Interfaces:**

- Consumes: `FilePicker` / `ScriptedPicker`, `AppModel.alert` / `report(_:title:)`, `DocumentModel.addImage`, `ImageResolver`, `GreyImageDecoder.inspect`, `UndoName` image names, `InspectorRequest`.
- Produces: `AppModel.performImage(_:on:using:)`, `AppModel.handleImage(_:) -> Task<Void, Never>?` (called by `handle(_ request:)`; `nil` before the window has given the app its panels, else the task doing the work); `DocumentModel.imageResolver`; `ContentType.png`, `.jpeg`; `DocumentParts(…, documentURL:)`; `save(to:)` writes `fileData(savingTo:)` and then calls `document.didSave(to:)` (Task 6), which sets `document.documentURL` and brings the linked paths into line; test fixtures `AppTestImages`, `AppTempFolder`.

**Note:** The two tests of the inspector's buttons reaching the app wait on exactly what they start: `handleImage` returns its `Task` (or `nil` when there are no panels), so the first awaits `task.value` and the second asserts that no task was started at all, instead of yielding a fixed number of times and hoping. `AppTempFolder` is in its own file (one type per file); the three temporary-folder fixtures (`TempFolder`, `TempFolderForNodes`, `AppTempFolder`) stay separate because a SwiftPM test target cannot import another's sources and a shared test-support target means editing `Package.swift`, which this track does not touch. Chosen files are read on the main actor (at most 16 MB + 1 byte); their decode happens later, off it, in the node. The link flow never writes beside an existing linked image: `Link Image…` asks the save panel where to put the bytes and writes exactly there. MetalUI's panels filter by `ContentType` extensions (like `.mcgraph`); FI-img-1 checks that a real panel greys out non-images and says what to do if it does not.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

`Tests/CreatorAppTests/ImageActionTests.swift` (new)

```swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorKernel
import CreatorNodes
import Foundation
import Testing
@testable import CreatorApp

/// The Image Field's buttons (fields spec §5): choosing, linking, relinking and embedding a picture through the file panels,
/// the refusals, and the file that results.
@MainActor
struct ImageActionTests {
    let folder = AppTempFolder()
    let picture = AppTestImages.png(level: 100)

    /// An app showing one Image Field, which is an output so that it is evaluated.
    func imageApp(_ setting: ImageReference? = nil) async -> (AppModel, Node) {
        var builder = GraphBuilder()
        let node = builder.add(ImageFieldNode.self)
        var graph = builder.graph
        graph.nodes[node.id]?.isOutput = true
        if let setting { graph.nodes[node.id]?.inputValues[NodeSetting.image] = .image(setting) }
        let app = await makeApp(graph)
        return (app, graph.nodes[node.id] ?? node)
    }

    func setting(_ app: AppModel, _ node: Node) -> ImageReference? {
        if case .image(let reference)? = app.document.graph.nodes[node.id]?.inputValues[NodeSetting.image] { reference } else { nil }
    }

    func state(_ app: AppModel, _ node: Node) async -> NodeState? {
        await app.settle()
        return app.document.results[node.id]?.state
    }

    // MARK: Choose

    @Test func choosingAFileEmbedsItAsOneUndoStepAndTheNodeRuns() async throws {
        let (app, node) = await imageApp()
        let file = try folder.write(picture, to: "vent.png")
        await app.performImage(.chooseImage, on: node.id, using: ScriptedPicker([file]))
        let hash = ImageHash(of: picture)
        #expect(setting(app, node) == .embedded(hash) && app.document.images[hash] == picture)
        #expect(app.isEdited && app.document.undoName == "Choose Image")
        #expect(await state(app, node)?.isSuccess == true)
        app.document.undo()
        #expect(setting(app, node) == nil && !app.isEdited)
    }

    @Test func thePanelIsAskedForPNGAndJPEGOnly() async throws {
        let (app, node) = await imageApp()
        let picker = ScriptedPicker([nil])
        await app.performImage(.chooseImage, on: node.id, using: picker)
        #expect(picker.asked.map(\.types) == [[.png, .jpeg]])
        #expect(app.alert == nil && setting(app, node) == nil, "cancelling changes nothing and says nothing")
    }

    @Test func aFileThatIsNotAUsableImageIsRefusedByNameAndTheSettingIsUnchanged() async throws {
        let (app, node) = await imageApp()
        let text = try folder.write(Data("not a picture".utf8), to: "notes.png")
        await app.performImage(.chooseImage, on: node.id, using: ScriptedPicker([text]))
        #expect(app.alert == .problem(AppProblem("“notes.png” can't be used as an image", ImageProblem.undecodable.message)))
        let gif = try folder.write(AppTestImages.png(type: .gif), to: "loop.gif")
        await app.performImage(.chooseImage, on: node.id, using: ScriptedPicker([gif]))
        #expect(app.alert == .problem(AppProblem("“loop.gif” can't be used as an image", ImageProblem.unsupportedType.message)))
        let wide = try folder.write(AppTestImages.png(8_193, 1), to: "wide.png")
        await app.performImage(.chooseImage, on: node.id, using: ScriptedPicker([wide]))
        #expect(app.alert == .problem(AppProblem("“wide.png” can't be used as an image",
                                                 ImageProblem.tooManyPixels(width: 8_193, height: 1).message)))
        #expect(setting(app, node) == nil && !app.isEdited && app.document.images.isEmpty)
    }

    @Test func aFileOverSixteenMegabytesIsRefusedWithoutBeingEmbedded() async throws {
        let (app, node) = await imageApp()
        let huge = try folder.write(Data(count: ImageLimits.maxBytes + 1), to: "huge.png")
        await app.performImage(.chooseImage, on: node.id, using: ScriptedPicker([huge]))
        #expect(app.alert == .problem(AppProblem("“huge.png” can't be used as an image",
                                                 ImageProblem.tooManyBytes(ImageLimits.maxBytes + 1).message)))
        #expect(setting(app, node) == nil && app.document.images.isEmpty)
    }

    @Test func aFolderOrAMissingFileChosenIsRefusedToo() async throws {
        let (app, node) = await imageApp()
        await app.performImage(.chooseImage, on: node.id, using: ScriptedPicker([folder.url]))
        await app.performImage(.chooseImage, on: node.id, using: ScriptedPicker([folder.url.appending(path: "gone.png")]))
        #expect(app.alert != nil && setting(app, node) == nil)
    }

    // MARK: Link

    @Test func linkingAnEmbeddedPictureSavesItWhereAskedThenLinksItWithoutTouchingAnythingElse() async throws {
        let hash = ImageHash(of: picture)
        let (app, node) = await imageApp(.embedded(hash))
        app.document.addImage(picture)
        let destination = folder.url.appending(path: "maps/vent.png")
        try FileManager.default.createDirectory(at: destination.deletingLastPathComponent(), withIntermediateDirectories: true)
        let picker = ScriptedPicker([destination])
        await app.performImage(.linkImage, on: node.id, using: picker)
        #expect(try Data(contentsOf: destination) == picture, "the original bytes")
        #expect(picker.asked.first?.name == "image.png")
        #expect(setting(app, node) == .linked(relativePath: nil, absolutePath: destination.standardizedFileURL.path(percentEncoded: false)))
        #expect(app.document.undoName == "Link Image")
        #expect(await state(app, node)?.isSuccess == true)
        #expect(try FileManager.default.contentsOfDirectory(atPath: folder.url.path(percentEncoded: false)) == ["maps"])
    }

    @Test func aWriteThatFailsLeavesTheEmbeddedPictureAlone() async throws {
        let hash = ImageHash(of: picture)
        let (app, node) = await imageApp(.embedded(hash))
        app.document.addImage(picture)
        let nowhere = folder.url.appending(path: "no/such/folder/vent.png")
        await app.performImage(.linkImage, on: node.id, using: ScriptedPicker([nowhere]))
        #expect(app.alert?.title == "“vent.png” couldn't be saved" && setting(app, node) == .embedded(hash))
    }

    @Test func withNoPictureLinkingAnExistingFileLinksIt() async throws {
        let (app, node) = await imageApp()
        let file = try folder.write(picture, to: "vent.png")
        await app.performImage(.linkImage, on: node.id, using: ScriptedPicker([file]))
        #expect(setting(app, node) == .linked(relativePath: nil, absolutePath: file.standardizedFileURL.path(percentEncoded: false)))
        #expect(app.document.undoName == "Link Image" && app.document.images.isEmpty, "nothing is copied into the document")
    }

    // MARK: Relink and embed

    @Test func relinkingAMissingFileFixesTheNodeAndOnceSavedRecordsAPathFromTheDocument() async throws {
        let oldPath = folder.url.appending(path: "old/vent.png").path(percentEncoded: false)
        let gone = ImageReference.linked(relativePath: nil, absolutePath: oldPath)
        let (app, node) = await imageApp(gone)
        #expect(await state(app, node) != nil)
        if case .error(let message)? = await state(app, node) {
            #expect(message.hasPrefix("Image 'vent.png' wasn't found at ") && message.hasSuffix("/old/. Relink it in the inspector."))
        } else {
            Issue.record("a missing link must be an error")
        }
        let documentURL = folder.url.appending(path: "doc/Plate.mcgraph")
        try FileManager.default.createDirectory(at: documentURL.deletingLastPathComponent(), withIntermediateDirectories: true)
        try app.save(to: documentURL)
        let file = try folder.write(picture, to: "doc/maps/vent.png")
        await app.performImage(.relinkImage, on: node.id, using: ScriptedPicker([file]))
        let absolute = file.standardizedFileURL.path(percentEncoded: false)
        #expect(setting(app, node) == .linked(relativePath: "maps/vent.png", absolutePath: absolute))
        #expect(app.document.undoName == "Relink Image")
        #expect(await state(app, node)?.isSuccess == true)
    }

    @Test func embeddingALinkReadsTheFileIntoTheDocumentAndUndoGoesBackToTheLink() async throws {
        let file = try folder.write(picture, to: "vent.png")
        let link = ImageReference.linked(relativePath: nil, absolutePath: file.path(percentEncoded: false))
        let (app, node) = await imageApp(link)
        await app.performImage(.embedImage, on: node.id, using: ScriptedPicker([]))
        #expect(setting(app, node) == .embedded(ImageHash(of: picture)) && app.document.images.count == 1)
        #expect(app.document.undoName == "Embed Image")
        app.document.undo()
        #expect(setting(app, node) == link)
    }

    @Test func embeddingALinkWhoseFileIsGoneSaysSoAndChangesNothing() async throws {
        let missing = folder.url.appending(path: "gone.png").path(percentEncoded: false)
        let link = ImageReference.linked(relativePath: nil, absolutePath: missing)
        let (app, node) = await imageApp(link)
        await app.performImage(.embedImage, on: node.id, using: ScriptedPicker([]))
        #expect(app.alert?.message.contains("wasn't found at") == true && setting(app, node) == link)
    }

    // MARK: The buttons reach the app

    @Test func anInspectorButtonPressedInTheEditorReachesTheAppShellAndRunsWithTheWindowsPanels() async throws {
        let (app, node) = await imageApp()
        let file = try folder.write(picture, to: "vent.png")
        app.filePicker = ScriptedPicker([file])
        app.editor.press(.chooseImage, on: node.id)
        let request = try #require(app.editor.inspectorRequest)
        let task = try #require(app.handleImage(request), "with the window's panels the button starts the action")
        await task.value
        #expect(setting(app, node) == .embedded(ImageHash(of: picture)))
    }

    @Test func withoutWindowPanelsTheButtonsStartNothing() async throws {
        let (app, node) = await imageApp()
        app.editor.press(.chooseImage, on: node.id)
        let request = try #require(app.editor.inspectorRequest)
        #expect(app.handleImage(request) == nil, "no task was started, so nothing can happen later either")
        app.handle(request)
        await app.settle()
        #expect(setting(app, node) == nil && app.alert == nil)
    }

    // MARK: The file

    @Test func savedAndReopenedAnEmbeddedPictureComesBackAndAnUnusedOneDoesNot() async throws {
        let (app, node) = await imageApp()
        let chosen = try folder.write(picture, to: "vent.png")
        await app.performImage(.chooseImage, on: node.id, using: ScriptedPicker([chosen]))
        app.document.addImage(AppTestImages.png(level: 7))
        let url = folder.url.appending(path: "Plate.mcgraph")
        try app.save(to: url)
        #expect(!app.isEdited && app.document.documentURL == url)

        let reopened = await makeApp()
        try reopened.open(url)
        await reopened.settle()
        #expect(reopened.document.images == [ImageHash(of: picture): picture], "the unused one was dropped on save")
        #expect(reopened.document.results[node.id]?.state.isSuccess == true)
        #expect(reopened.document.documentURL == url)
    }

    @Test func aDocumentFolderMovedWithItsLinkedPictureStillFindsItThroughTheRelativePath() async throws {
        let (app, node) = await imageApp()
        let original = folder.url.appending(path: "first", directoryHint: .isDirectory)
        let file = try folder.write(picture, to: "first/maps/vent.png")
        await app.performImage(.linkImage, on: node.id, using: ScriptedPicker([file]))
        let url = original.appending(path: "Plate.mcgraph")
        try app.save(to: url)
        let saved = try GraphFileIO.decode(Data(contentsOf: url), registry: app.registry)
        let stored = saved.graph.nodes[node.id]?.inputValues[NodeSetting.image]
        let absolute = file.standardizedFileURL.path(percentEncoded: false)
        #expect(stored == .image(.linked(relativePath: "maps/vent.png", absolutePath: absolute)))

        let moved = folder.url.appending(path: "second", directoryHint: .isDirectory)
        try FileManager.default.moveItem(at: original, to: moved)
        let reopened = await makeApp()
        try reopened.open(moved.appending(path: "Plate.mcgraph"))
        await reopened.settle()
        #expect(reopened.document.results[node.id]?.state.isSuccess == true, "the absolute path is stale; the relative one is not")
    }

    /// After Save As into another folder the open document is on the new folder's terms at once, not at the next reopen:
    /// the relative path that was tried first is made again from where the file is, so a same-named picture in the new
    /// folder cannot take its place unseen; and saving is no edit and no undo step.
    @Test func afterSaveAsTheOpenDocumentIgnoresALookAlikePictureInTheNewFolder() async throws {
        let (app, node) = await imageApp()
        let file = try folder.write(picture, to: "first/maps/vent.png")
        await app.performImage(.linkImage, on: node.id, using: ScriptedPicker([file]))
        try app.save(to: folder.url.appending(path: "first/Plate.mcgraph"))
        let lookAlike = try folder.write(AppTestImages.png(level: 200), to: "second/maps/vent.png")
        let steps = app.document.undoName
        try app.save(to: folder.url.appending(path: "second/Plate.mcgraph"))
        let absolute = file.standardizedFileURL.path(percentEncoded: false)
        #expect(setting(app, node) == .linked(relativePath: "../first/maps/vent.png", absolutePath: absolute))
        #expect(app.document.imageResolver.location(of: .linked(relativePath: "../first/maps/vent.png", absolutePath: absolute))
            == file.standardizedFileURL)
        #expect(!app.isEdited && app.document.undoName == steps, "saving is no edit and adds no undo step")
        #expect(try Data(contentsOf: lookAlike) != picture)
        await app.settle()
        let scalar = try #require(app.document.results[node.id]?.outputs?["field"]?.items.first)
        guard case .field(.image(let image, _, _, _, _)) = scalar else {
            Issue.record("the node should make an image field")
            return
        }
        #expect(image.pixels.allSatisfy { $0 == 100 * 257 }, "the picture beside the first folder, not the look-alike")
    }
}
```

```swift
// Test fixture file: small PNGs for the tests that choose, link and embed pictures through the app.
import CoreGraphics
import Foundation
import ImageIO
import UniformTypeIdentifiers

enum AppTestImages {
    /// A `width` × `height` PNG of one grey level (0…255).
    static func png(_ width: Int = 4, _ height: Int = 4, level: Double = 128, type: UTType = .png) -> Data {
        guard let context = CGContext(data: nil, width: width, height: height, bitsPerComponent: 8, bytesPerRow: width,
                                      space: CGColorSpaceCreateDeviceGray(), bitmapInfo: CGImageAlphaInfo.none.rawValue),
              let image = { () -> CGImage? in
                  context.setFillColor(gray: level / 255, alpha: 1)
                  context.fill(CGRect(x: 0, y: 0, width: width, height: height))
                  return context.makeImage()
              }() else { return Data() }
        let data = NSMutableData()
        guard let destination = CGImageDestinationCreateWithData(data, type.identifier as CFString, 1, nil) else { return Data() }
        CGImageDestinationAddImage(destination, image, nil)
        CGImageDestinationFinalize(destination)
        return data as Data
    }
}
```

```swift
// Test fixture file: a folder that removes itself, for the files an app test links to.
import Foundation

/// A folder that removes itself, for the files an app test links to.
final class AppTempFolder {
    let url = URL.temporaryDirectory.appending(path: "mc-app-\(UUID().uuidString)", directoryHint: .isDirectory)

    init() { try? FileManager.default.createDirectory(at: url, withIntermediateDirectories: true) }
    deinit { try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: url) }

    @discardableResult
    func write(_ data: Data, to name: String) throws -> URL {
        let file = url.appending(path: name)
        try FileManager.default.createDirectory(at: file.deletingLastPathComponent(), withIntermediateDirectories: true)
        try data.write(to: file)
        return file
    }
}
```

`Sources/CreatorApp/AppModel+Images.swift` (new file)

```swift
import CreatorEditor
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorKernel
import CreatorNodes
import Foundation
import MetalUI

extension AppModel {
    /// The file types an Image Field reads (fields spec §10: PNG and JPEG).
    static let imageTypes: [ContentType] = [.png, .jpeg]

    /// An image button of the inspector (`EditorModel.inspectorRequest`): the app shell owns the file panels, so it carries
    /// these out. Nothing happens before the window has given the app its panels (`nil`); otherwise the task doing it is
    /// returned, so a test can wait for exactly that and nothing else.
    @discardableResult
    func handleImage(_ request: InspectorRequest) -> Task<Void, Never>? {
        guard let filePicker else { return nil }
        return Task { await performImage(request.action, on: request.node, using: filePicker) }
    }

    /// Carries out an image button for the Image Field `node` on the level shown (fields spec §5), as one undo step, or tells
    /// the person why not and changes nothing:
    /// - `chooseImage`: a file is chosen and **embedded**;
    /// - `linkImage`: an embedded picture is saved to a file the person chooses and the node links to it; with no picture, an
    ///   existing file is linked;
    /// - `relinkImage`: another file for a link (the node's error offers this when the file is gone);
    /// - `embedImage`: the linked file is read into the document.
    /// A file is checked when it is chosen (`ImageResolver.bytes(ofFileAt:)`, `GreyImageDecoder.inspect`): over 16 MB, over
    /// 8,192 px on a side, or not a PNG or JPEG, and the setting stays as it was (fields spec D10).
    public func performImage(_ action: InspectorAction, on node: NodeID, using picker: any FilePicker) async {
        editor.commitPendingEntry()
        guard editor.graph.nodes[node] != nil else { return }
        do {
            switch action {
            case .chooseImage: try await chooseImage(for: node, using: picker)
            case .linkImage: try await linkImage(for: node, using: picker)
            case .relinkImage: try await relinkImage(for: node, using: picker, named: UndoName.relinkImage)
            case .embedImage: try embedImage(for: node)
            case .pickEdgesInView, .pickFacesInView, .editSketch, .editGroup, .makeUnique, .ungroup: break
            }
        } catch let problem as AppProblem {
            alert = .problem(problem)
        } catch let problem as ImageProblem {
            alert = .problem(AppProblem("That image can't be used", problem.message))
        } catch {
            report(error, title: "The image couldn't be changed")
        }
    }

    private func currentImage(of node: NodeID) -> ImageReference? {
        if case .image(let reference)? = editor.graph.nodes[node]?.inputValues[NodeSetting.image] { reference } else { nil }
    }

    private func chooseImage(for node: NodeID, using picker: any FilePicker) async throws {
        guard let url = try await picker.chooseFileToOpen(Self.imageTypes) else { return }
        let bytes = try checkedBytes(of: url)
        try setImage(.embedded(document.addImage(bytes)), on: node, named: UndoName.chooseImage)
    }

    private func linkImage(for node: NodeID, using picker: any FilePicker) async throws {
        guard case .embedded(let hash)? = currentImage(of: node) else {
            return try await relinkImage(for: node, using: picker, named: UndoName.linkImage)
        }
        guard let bytes = document.images[hash] else { throw ImageProblem.embeddedMissing }
        let format = try GreyImageDecoder.inspect(bytes).format
        guard let url = try await picker.chooseDestination(Self.imageTypes, defaultName: "image.\(format.fileExtension)") else { return }
        do {
            try bytes.write(to: url, options: .atomic)
        } catch {
            throw AppProblem("“\(url.lastPathComponent)” couldn't be saved", error.localizedDescription)
        }
        try setImage(linkedReference(to: url), on: node, named: UndoName.linkImage)
    }

    private func relinkImage(for node: NodeID, using picker: any FilePicker, named name: String) async throws {
        guard let url = try await picker.chooseFileToOpen(Self.imageTypes) else { return }
        _ = try checkedBytes(of: url)
        try setImage(linkedReference(to: url), on: node, named: name)
    }

    private func embedImage(for node: NodeID) throws {
        guard let reference = currentImage(of: node), !reference.isEmbedded else { return }
        let bytes = try document.imageResolver.bytes(of: reference)
        _ = try GreyImageDecoder.inspect(bytes)
        try setImage(.embedded(document.addImage(bytes)), on: node, named: UndoName.embedImage)
    }

    /// The bytes of the file at `url` once they pass the image limits, else a sentence naming the file.
    private func checkedBytes(of url: URL) throws -> Data {
        do {
            let bytes = try ImageResolver.bytes(ofFileAt: url)
            _ = try GreyImageDecoder.inspect(bytes)
            return bytes
        } catch {
            throw AppProblem("“\(url.lastPathComponent)” can't be used as an image", error.message)
        }
    }

    /// A link to `url`: its absolute path and, once the document has a folder, the path relative to it.
    private func linkedReference(to url: URL) -> ImageReference {
        let file = url.standardizedFileURL
        let relative = document.documentURL.map { ImageResolver.relativePath(from: $0.deletingLastPathComponent(), to: file) }
        return .linked(relativePath: relative, absolutePath: file.path(percentEncoded: false))
    }

    private func setImage(_ reference: ImageReference, on node: NodeID, named name: String) throws {
        do {
            try document.perform(.setInput(node, NodeSetting.image, .image(reference)), at: editor.graphPath, name: name)
        } catch {
            throw AppProblem("The image couldn't be changed", error.message)
        }
    }
}
```

### Task 10: Acceptance, the group case, and the docs

The end-to-end check the spec's §8 asks of images: a vented plate whose hole sizes follow a picture (Image Field → Remap → Sample Field → Hole Pattern, on OCCT), the same document saved and reopened with identical results, a linked picture edited on disk, a missing picture, and an Image Field inside a group. Then CLAUDE.md, the spec's Errata, and human-check group FI-img (orientation, a large picture, overwriting through Link Image, an EXIF-rotated photo, and Save As beside a look-alike, besides the choose / link / relink / embed flows), and the one line of group TR that said a save writes format 6.

**Files:**

- Modify: `CLAUDE.md`
- Modify: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-12-fields-design.md`
- Modify: `docs/verification/human-checks.md`

Tests:

- Create: `Tests/CreatorGraphTests/Images/ImageGroupTests.swift`
- Create: `Tests/CreatorNodesTests/Fields/ImageAcceptanceTests.swift`

**Interfaces:**

- Consumes: Everything above; `PatternPlate`, `Harness`, `volume(_:_:)`, `onlySolid`.
- Produces: no new API; documentation: CLAUDE.md, `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-12-fields-design.md` (Errata 7b-2), `docs/verification/human-checks.md` (group FI-img).

**Note:** `docs/metalui-gaps.md` is **not** edited: no gap was hit (the file panels, `ContentType` filtering by extension, the inspector's `.button` and `.readOnly` rows all exist). Check FI-img-1 says what to log if a real panel lets a non-image through.

```swift
import CreatorKernel
import Foundation
import Testing
@testable import CreatorGraph

/// An image used inside a group definition (fields spec §5): the group node runs it with the document's images and folder, and a
/// file edited on disk reaches it as it reaches a node on the top level.
struct ImageGroupTests {
    let folder = TempFolder()

    /// "Probe": no inputs, one integer output `size`, with an Image Probe inside reading `reference`.
    func probeGroup(_ reference: ImageReference) -> (GroupDefinition, Node) {
        var inner = EvaluatorImageTests.registry.makeNode(ImageProbeNode.typeID)
        inner.inputValues[NodeSetting.image] = .image(reference)
        let definition = define("Probe", outputs: [SocketSpec("size", .integer)], nodes: [inner]) { _, output in
            [link(inner, "size", output, "size")]
        }
        return (definition, inner)
    }

    func size(_ report: EvaluationReport, _ node: Node) -> Int? {
        if case .one(.integer(let value))? = report.results[node.id]?.outputs?["size"] { value } else { nil }
    }

    @Test func aGroupRunsItsImageFromTheDocumentsPoolAndRunsAgainWhenALinkedFileChanges() async throws {
        let data = Data("embedded bytes".utf8)
        let (embedded, _) = probeGroup(.embedded(ImageHash(of: data)))
        let embeddedGroup = instance(of: embedded)
        let evaluator = Evaluator(registry: EvaluatorImageTests.registry, kernel: FakeKernel())
        let held = ImageResolver(embedded: [ImageHash(of: data): data])
        let first = try await evaluator.evaluate(graph([embeddedGroup]), definitions: table([embedded]), demand: [embeddedGroup.id],
                                                 images: held)
        #expect(size(first, embeddedGroup) == 14)

        let file = try folder.write(Data(count: 5), to: "vent.png")
        try folder.touch(file, Date(timeIntervalSince1970: 1_000))
        let (linked, _) = probeGroup(.linked(relativePath: nil, absolutePath: file.path(percentEncoded: false)))
        let linkedGroup = instance(of: linked)
        let before = try await evaluator.evaluate(graph([linkedGroup]), definitions: table([linked]), demand: [linkedGroup.id])
        #expect(size(before, linkedGroup) == 5)
        #expect(try await evaluator.evaluate(graph([linkedGroup]), definitions: table([linked]), demand: [linkedGroup.id])
            .evaluatedNodes.isEmpty, "unchanged: cached")
        try Data(count: 9).write(to: file)
        try folder.touch(file, Date(timeIntervalSince1970: 2_000))
        let after = try await evaluator.evaluate(graph([linkedGroup]), definitions: table([linked]), demand: [linkedGroup.id])
        #expect(size(after, linkedGroup) == 9, "the node inside the group saw the new date")
    }
}
```

`Tests/CreatorNodesTests/Fields/ImageAcceptanceTests.swift` (new)

```swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorKernel
import CreatorNodes
import CreatorOCCT
import Foundation
import Testing

/// Image-driven patterns (fields spec §1 purpose 3, §8): a grey picture, projected on the plate, sizes the holes: Image Field →
/// Remap → Sample Field → a Hole Pattern's `diameter`, on OCCT, then the same document saved, reopened, linked and edited on disk.
@MainActor
struct ImageAcceptanceTests {
    let folder = TempFolderForNodes()
    /// 4 × 1 px, black to white left to right. Over the 60 mm plate, clamped, the grey at x is `position / 3` with
    /// `position = clamp((x / 60 + 0.5) × 4 − 0.5, 0, 3)`; Remap turns grey 0…1 into Ø3…Ø5.
    let ramp = TestImages.grey(4, 1) { x, _ in x * 85 }

    struct Part {
        var plate = PatternPlate()
        var sample: Node
        var holes: Node
        var remap: Node
        var image: Node
    }

    func part(_ reference: ImageReference?) -> Part {
        var plate = PatternPlate()
        var values: [SocketName: ConstantValue] = ["width": .number(60), "height": .number(40), "edges": .integer(1)]
        if let reference { values[NodeSetting.image] = .image(reference) }
        let image = plate.h.add(ImageFieldNode.self, values)
        let remap = plate.h.add(FieldRemapNode.self, [
            "fromLow": .number(0), "fromHigh": .number(1), "toLow": .number(3), "toHigh": .number(5),
        ])
        let sample = plate.h.add(SampleFieldNode.self)
        let holes = plate.h.add(HolePatternNode.self, ["depth": .number(6)], output: true)
        plate.h.wire(image, "field", to: remap, "field")
        plate.h.wire(remap, "field", to: sample, "field")
        plate.h.wire(plate.placements, "placements", to: sample, "at")
        plate.h.wire(sample, "values", to: holes, "diameter")
        plate.h.wire(plate.plate, "solid", to: holes, "part")
        plate.h.wire(plate.placements, "placements", to: holes, "placements")
        return Part(plate: plate, sample: sample, holes: holes, remap: remap, image: image)
    }

    func diameter(atX x: Double, reversed: Bool = false) -> Double {
        let position = min(max((x / 60 + 0.5) * 4 - 0.5, 0), 3)
        return 3 + 2 * (reversed ? 1 - position / 3 : position / 3)
    }

    func origins(_ placements: Value?) -> [Vector3] {
        (placements?.items ?? []).compactMap { item in if case .plane(let plane) = item { plane.origin } else { nil } }
    }

    @Test func aPictureSizesTheHolesAndTheHolesCutAreThoseSizes() async throws {
        let kernel = OCCTKernel()
        let hash = ImageHash(of: ramp)
        let part = part(.embedded(hash))
        let report = try await part.plate.h.run([part.holes], kernel: kernel, images: ImageResolver(embedded: [hash: ramp]))
        #expect(report.isOK(part.image) && report.isOK(part.sample) && report.isOK(part.holes),
                "\(String(describing: report.state(part.holes)))")
        let at = origins(report.value(part.plate.placements, "placements"))
        let sizes = try #require(report.value(part.sample, "values")?.numbers)
        #expect(at.count == 4 && sizes.count == 4)
        #expect(zip(sizes, at).allSatisfy { abs($0 - diameter(atX: $1.x)) < 1e-9 }, "got \(sizes)")
        let rounded = sizes.sorted().map { ($0 * 1000).rounded() / 1000 }
        #expect(rounded == [3.111, 3.111, 4.889, 4.889], "darker on the left, lighter on the right")
        let want = sizes.reduce(0) { $0 + PatternPlate.cylinderVolume($1, 6) }
        let removed = PatternPlate.plateVolume - (try await volume(try onlySolid(report, part.holes), kernel))
        #expect(isClose(removed, want), "removed \(removed), wanted \(want)")
    }

    @Test func theSavedDocumentReopensToTheSameHolesOnASecondRun() async throws {
        let kernel = OCCTKernel()
        let hash = ImageHash(of: ramp)
        let part = part(.embedded(hash))
        let document = DocumentModel(file: GraphFile(graph: part.plate.h.graph, images: [hash: ramp]),
                                     registry: BuiltInNodes.registry, kernel: kernel)
        await document.waitForEvaluation()
        let first = try #require(document.results[part.sample.id]?.outputs?["values"]?.numbers)
        let data = try document.fileData()
        #expect(try #require(String(bytes: data, encoding: .utf8)).contains("\"formatVersion\" : 7"))
        let reopened = try DocumentModel(data: data, registry: BuiltInNodes.registry, kernel: kernel)
        await reopened.waitForEvaluation()
        #expect(reopened.results[part.sample.id]?.outputs?["values"]?.numbers == first, "identical on a second run")
        #expect(reopened.results[part.holes.id]?.state.isSuccess == true)
        let solid = try #require(reopened.results[part.holes.id]?.outputs?["solid"]?.solids?.first)
        let want = first.reduce(0) { $0 + PatternPlate.cylinderVolume($1, 6) }
        #expect(isClose(PatternPlate.plateVolume - (try await volume(solid, kernel)), want))
    }

    @Test func aLinkedPictureEditedOnDiskResizesTheHolesAtTheNextEvaluation() async throws {
        let kernel = OCCTKernel()
        let file = try folder.write(ramp, to: "maps/ramp.png")
        try folder.touch(file, Date(timeIntervalSince1970: 1_000))
        let part = part(.linked(relativePath: "maps/ramp.png", absolutePath: "/no/such/ramp.png"))
        let document = DocumentModel(file: GraphFile(graph: part.plate.h.graph), registry: BuiltInNodes.registry, kernel: kernel,
                                     documentURL: folder.url.appending(path: "Plate.mcgraph"))
        await document.waitForEvaluation()
        let before = try #require(document.results[part.sample.id]?.outputs?["values"]?.numbers)
        let at = origins(document.results[part.plate.placements.id]?.outputs?["placements"])
        #expect(zip(before, at).allSatisfy { abs($0 - diameter(atX: $1.x)) < 1e-9 })

        try folder.write(TestImages.grey(4, 1) { x, _ in 255 - x * 85 }, to: "maps/ramp.png")
        try folder.touch(file, Date(timeIntervalSince1970: 2_000))
        try document.perform(.setInput(part.remap.id, "toHigh", .number(5)), name: "Change To High")  // any edit evaluates again
        await document.waitForEvaluation()
        let after = try #require(document.results[part.sample.id]?.outputs?["values"]?.numbers)
        #expect(zip(after, at).allSatisfy { abs($0 - diameter(atX: $1.x, reversed: true)) < 1e-9 }, "the picture on disk is read again")
    }

    @Test func aMissingPictureStopsTheChainAtTheImageWithoutHarmingAnythingElse() async throws {
        let kernel = OCCTKernel()
        let path = folder.url.appending(path: "textures/vent-mask.png").path(percentEncoded: false)
        let part = part(.linked(relativePath: nil, absolutePath: path))
        let report = try await part.plate.h.run([part.holes], kernel: kernel)
        let message = try #require(report.error(part.image))
        #expect(message.hasPrefix("Image 'vent-mask.png' wasn't found at ") && message.hasSuffix("/textures/. Relink it in the inspector."))
        #expect(report.isOK(part.plate.plate) && report.isOK(part.plate.placements))
        #expect(report.value(part.holes, "solid") == nil, "no holes without sizes")
        let healed = try folder.write(ramp, to: "textures/vent-mask.png")
        #expect(FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: healed.path(percentEncoded: false)))
        let again = try await part.plate.h.run([part.holes], kernel: kernel)
        #expect(again.isOK(part.holes), "the file appeared: the next evaluation uses it")
    }
}
```

```diff
@@ -254,3 +254,36 @@ as PNG and JPEG.
   plate's per point; `field-2b` records it without a budget.
 - **The distance cache (§2.2) counts the solids it keeps alive** (`Solid.estimatedBytes`) with its points and distances, so its
   64 MB bounds what it pins.
+
+## Errata (7b-2: images and file format 7)
+
+- **The `images` table is a JSON object (§5).** `"images": {"<sha256 hex>": "<base64>"}`, keys sorted by the encoder, written only when
+  a node uses an embedded picture. Reading is forgiving: an entry whose key is not 64 lowercase hex digits, whose text is not base64,
+  whose bytes pass 16 MB or do not hash to the key is dropped, and so is an entry whose value is not a string; each is judged alone, so
+  one bad picture costs only itself. A node that points at a dropped one fails with "This embedded image's data is missing from the
+  file, or damaged. Choose the image again in the inspector." The rest of the document opens; the next save writes the table without
+  the dropped entry, for good.
+- **A linked image's relative path is optional (§5).** `.linked(relativePath: String?, absolutePath: String)`. A save writes both paths
+  for a file it can find, the relative one made from the folder being saved to (so Save As elsewhere makes it again), and leaves a
+  missing file's paths as they were. The relative path is tried first, then the absolute one.
+- **Limits apply to every picture (D10).** A linked file over 16 MB or 8,192 px a side, or not a PNG or JPEG, fails the node like an
+  embedded one; D10 said "embedded". A linked path that is not a regular file (folder, device, pipe) is refused before it is opened.
+- **A picture that cannot be decoded is remembered under its stamp (§5).** A cut-short or damaged file that is unchanged fails at once at
+  every evaluation instead of being read and decoded again each time (at most 256 are remembered); a changed stamp tries it again. A
+  read error is not remembered: fixing a permission changes no stamp.
+- **A file is opened without blocking and checked again (§5).** The path is checked to be a regular file before it is opened, and the
+  open descriptor is checked once more, so a path swapped for a named pipe in between cannot hold the evaluation.
+- **Save As brings the open document along (§5).** After a save the document's linked paths are the ones just written (no undo step, no
+  edit), so a same-named picture in the new folder cannot take the linked one's place unseen.
+- **`image` is no reserved socket name.** `NodeSetting.image` is left out of `NodeSetting.reservedForSockets` (the names a group socket or an exposed Sketch dimension may not take; it is in `NodeSetting.all`): only Image Field has it, and a group
+  socket or an exposed Sketch dimension called "image" in an older file keeps working.
+- **Truncated PNGs are caught by structure (§5).** ImageIO decodes a PNG cut short as an all-black picture without an error, so a PNG is
+  only accepted when its chunks lead to IEND (`PNGStructure`); a cut-short JPEG is caught by ImageIO's own status.
+- **The decode cache is keyed by what the reference points at, not by content hash (§5).** An embedded picture's key is its hash; a
+  linked file's is path, modification date and size, so the file is not read again while it is unchanged (D11).
+- **Colour and transparency (§5, §10).** A colour picture is accepted and converted (Rec. 709 luminance of the colour composited against
+  black); only the node's recipe says it was not grey. A JPEG or PNG's EXIF orientation is applied (U11 (b)).
+- **`Field.ownPlane` (§6).** The plane of the first linear gradient or image in a field, searched depth first from the left, for the slice
+  plane to start on; fields with neither (distance, radial, noise, constants) have none.
+- **Format 7 lifts the group interface's `field` refusal (7b-1 Errata).** `GroupRefusal.fieldSocket` is gone; a field wire may cross a
+  group's edge and an interface socket may be a field.
```

```diff
+  adds it once). Link it again, save, then move the folder holding the document and the picture to another place and open the document
+  there: the holes are as before (the relative path works while the stored absolute one is stale). Then Save As into a new folder that

+- [ ] **FI-img-8 Which way up.** Use a 4 × 4 Grid Points and the top-to-bottom ramp, with the view from above (the cube's Top face) and the
+  plane left on XY: the holes at the far side (+y) are the smallest and the near side the largest, and a left-to-right ramp makes the
+  left holes smallest. Now look from underneath (the cube's Bottom face): the pattern reads mirrored. That is right: the picture is projected
+  along the plane's normal and reads correctly from the normal's side. A horizontal flip or a vertical flip from above is a defect (the
+  pictures' row 0 is the plane's +y). Pinned: `ImageFieldSamplingTests`, `ImageFieldNodeTests`. **Observed:**

+  each finishes in a few seconds (note the time) with Activity Monitor's memory for the app staying under about 1.5 GB; editing the Hole
```

## Risks

- **ImageIO is the platform's.** Colour management and the handling of damaged files can differ between OS versions (a colour picture can shift a count or two of 65,535; the truncated-PNG-as-black behaviour was found by a failing test and is guarded by our own chunk walk, but another ImageIO quirk may exist). The decoder's tests pin what this OS does; a new OS may need an adjustment, and the colour tests allow ±2.
- **Memory.** The largest legal picture (8,192²) decodes to a 128 MB grey result. Measured peak footprints (Task 3, release): 239 to 306 MB for grey PNGs, 677 to 711 MB for the largest colour PNGs (ImageIO holds the decoded colour picture while the bands are drawn from it), 210 MB for a colour JPEG; the debug test process for the flat grey peaks near 400 MB. Two such pictures decoding at once, plus `GreyImageCache` (256 MB) and the result cache's 512 MB budget (which counts a field's image bytes), can reach well over 1 GB. A 16 MB PNG of 8,192² compresses well, so this is reachable from a small file. No cap on decoded bytes by format was added (user decision U7 and its follow-up); nothing limits how many pictures a document may embed (U10): twenty 16 MB pictures are a 430 MB JSON file.
- **Decoding runs inside the evaluation.** A big picture holds up that evaluation, though it is cancellable between bands and never on the main actor: in release, about 0.2 to 0.5 s for the largest grey picture, 1.4 to 3 s for the largest colour PNG and 0.7 s for a colour JPEG (Task 3's table, measured on a busy Mac); about 9 s for the flat grey test in a debug build on an idle one. A later task could decode ahead of time. A picture that cannot be decoded is not retried while its file is unchanged (Task 5).
- **Timestamps.** A file rewritten with the same size and the same modification time is not noticed (D11's rule is the date). No file watcher: a change on disk is seen at the next evaluation, not when the window comes forward (FI-img-4).
- **Save As and undo.** `DocumentModel.didSave(to:)` rewrites the open document's linked paths after a save without an undo step, so a same-named picture in the new folder cannot take the linked one's place. An undo of an earlier Link or Relink step puts back that step's own stored paths (which may be a relative path from the old folder) until the next save writes them again; the effect is the same silent look-alike for a person who undoes across a Save As, and it is accepted as rare.
- **Privacy in shared files.** An absolute fallback path (U4) and an embedded JPEG's EXIF (U5) travel inside the `.mcgraph`.
- **The name `image`.** `NodeSetting.all` lists `image`, but `NodeSetting.reservedForSockets` (read by `GroupNaming.isReserved` and `SketchSockets.isReserved`) leaves it out, so a sketch with an exposed dimension, or a group with a socket, called "image" in an existing file keeps working as before (Task 1). A future node that has an `image` setting *and* exposes sockets would have to reserve it.
- **Pool growth.** `DocumentModel.images` keeps every picture embedded or pasted in the session so undo works; it is trimmed only by a save-and-reopen.
- **Shared files with 7b-3** (listed at the top): the second merger recounts the catalog (66, `.fields: 14`), keeps both Fields library entries and both human-check groups.
- **Tests that are heavy.** `theLargestAllowedPictureDecodesInBands` adds about 9 s (22 s on a heavily loaded Mac) and 400 MB to a debug `swift test`; the suite runs it once.
- **Stat calls on the evaluator's actor.** Each evaluation takes up to three `stat`-family calls per image node (`ImageResolver.stamp`: the relative path, the symbolic-link resolution, the attributes) inside the `Evaluator` actor. A hung network volume or a dataless iCloud file can hold every evaluation up for as long as the system call blocks, whichever node is being edited. Reading is guarded (a non-blocking open and an `fstat` on the descriptor, so a path swapped for a pipe cannot block); the stats cannot be made non-blocking. If it bites: take the stamps once per evaluation, off the actor, with a timeout.
- **Remembered failures follow the stamp.** A damaged file replaced by different bytes with the same size and modification time is not retried (the same rule as D11 for a good file); touching it, or saving it again, is. A read error is never remembered.
- **A dropped `images` entry is gone on the next save** (U2): the file on disk keeps it until then.
